    readonly readyMs?: number;

One-character-class typo; fixed + 16 vector tests (incl. node:crypto cross-checks) now green. Debug scripts removed after use.
- Fixed 2 latent test bugs (never run before interruption): controller.dirty is the cached view as of the last project.summary read (refreshes each tick), so the post-edit assertion now checks the editor's own state; the transient-failures test needed a fresh edit before its 7th upload (cadence-resumed assertion).
- Engine hardening (real upstream semantics verified): upstream file.save* calls mark_saved() so project.summary flips dirty->clean the moment we persist locally — saveOnce now sets proactiveDirty ("the cloud is owed this save") after passing the dirty gate, so failed-upload retries never silently no-op on the flipped state; cleared on success/dedup. This also keeps controller.dirty truthful while uploads fail.
- Stabilized fake-timer tests under parallel-worker CPU contention: the save chain crosses a REAL async seam (jsdom WebCrypto subtle.digest resolves on Node's threadpool) — pump/tickUntilUploaded/wait loops now interleave realSleep(1) via node:timers/promises (bypasses faked globals). 4 consecutive full-suite runs: 62/62.

  loadProjectSession({bridge, client, projectId}) -> {openedPath, activeComp, missingAssets[], assetPaths};
  createAutosaveController({bridge, client, projectId, baseVersion, hooks?, intervalMs?=30s(D-6), maxConsecutiveErrors?=5, immediateRetries?=2}) -> {get dirty, markDirty, saveNow(reason)->SaveOutcome, dispose};

- sha256Hex is exported for the page's upload seam if it wants to pre-compute (uses WebCrypto with a verified sync fallback).

Task ID: M4-C
Agent: M4-C (web context collector — Seam 5)
Task: apps/web/src/lib/chat/{context.ts, tokens.ts} — bridge-driven project/comp/selection/playhead snapshotting with per-block token estimates + user-controllable flags, deterministic tests against the fake editor bridge.

Work Log:
- Read worklog tail, M4-SEAMS.md (binding; Seam 5 owned), AGENTS.md, ECUX §5 + §3.2, PA §8.3; verified the bridge runtime surface READ-ONLY: editor-bridge types.ts (EditorBridge, UiInspect, ProjectSummary, LayerNode/PropNode), fake.ts command registry + query impls, and cross-checked every query param name against editor/crates/engine/src/commands/query.rs (+ mod.rs comp_id/layer_p/resolve_layer, edit.rs history_list).
- VERIFIED QUERY PARAM NAMES (fake + real engine agree; needed by M4-C2 + wave-2):
  - project.summary: {} (no params) → {path, dirty, items[], activeComp, time, selectedLayers[], undo[]}
  - comp.info: { comp? } — comp = item id (number) or name (string); DEFAULT the active comp
  - layer.tree: { layer?, comp?, depth?, time? } — layer = id (number; also "#n"/name upstream), DEFAULT the FIRST SELECTED layer; depth default 16 upstream (ignored by fake); returns ONE layer node {…layerJson, time, properties} (single-layer "Layer Property Tree", NOT a whole-comp tree)
  - prop.get: { layer?, path } — `path` REQUIRED (alias `prop`); layer default the first selected; missing property rejects "invalid parameters for `prop.get`: layer N has no property `X`"
  - edit.history.list: {} → { states: [{index,id,label,parent,depth,current,line,future}], current, branches }
  - KEY FACT: ui.inspect carries RAW comp seconds in `time` (control.rs: `"time": app.session.time().seconds()`), no formatted timecode → collector formats its own mm:ss.d label.
- Built tokens.ts: estimateTokens (JSON.stringify length/4, floor 1 for non-empty, 0 for null/undefined/""; never throws — handles JSON.stringify returning undefined for functions + throwing for circular refs) + formatTokenEstimate ("~420 tokens" / "~1.2k tokens", negatives clamped to 0).
- Built context.ts: ContextBlock {kind: keyof ContextSelection, label, tokenEstimate, data?: unknown}, ContextSnapshot {flags, blocks, pinned, totalTokenEstimate}, DEFAULT_FLAGS (§5.1: projectSummary/activeComp/playhead/focusedPanel true; selection/selectedProperties/currentFrame/recentCommands/comments false), autoFlags (selection-derived flags on iff a selection exists; accepts the inspect result or null), timecodeLabel (mm:ss.d), commentsBlockPlaceholder (gateway-fed block, data undefined; M4-J wires the fetch), collectContextSnapshot (see signatures below).
- collectContextSnapshot design: ONE shared inspect feeds selection/playhead/focusedPanel/activeComp; blocks in ContextSelection key order; every off flag absent; selection/selectedProperties with NO selection → absent (§5.1 "when a selection exists"); selection reads layer.tree per selected layer (explicit `layer` id + `comp` + depth:2, capped at 8, `truncated` flag in data when more selected — NOTE: the engine defaults `layer` to the FIRST selected layer, so the seam's bare {depth:2, comp} shape would silently drop all but the first; per-layer calls are the faithful "Selection: <names>" implementation and stay registry-verified); selectedProperties reads the FIRST selected layer only, 6 candidate paths (transform/anchor|position|scale|rotation|opacity + text/sourceText) capped at 12, bounded concurrency 4 via a private mapLimit; per-item nulls on missing layer/property; recentCommands trims edit.history.list states to the last 10 (rest verbatim); currentFrame = data-free "Frame preview" chip (the TOOL EXECUTOR renders on demand); failures degrade to `data: undefined` + " (unavailable)" label suffix, never throw (last-resort catch returns a flags+empty-blocks snapshot); totalTokenEstimate = blocks + pinned chips (estimateTokens per PinnedContextItem).
- Tests (20 + 7 = 27 new, all deterministic, real timers, no network): default+auto flags with/without selection (incl. real bridge.inspect input); happy all-flags-on (9 blocks, exact labels, data shapes, estimates = estimateTokens(data), total = blocks+pinned); flags off → absent; no-selection → selection-derived blocks absent; multi-layer selection via wrapped inspect (per-item null for an unknown layer, first-layer-only properties); >8 selection cap + truncated; prop.get partial failure → per-item nulls; rejecting query (fake failOn) → " (unavailable)"; broken inspect → all ui-derived blocks degrade while comp.info still resolves via the engine's active comp; no-comp-open → "Comp (unavailable)"; history trim to last 10 (12 journaled edits → states[0].index 3, current 12); currentFrame placeholder (no data key); comments placeholder; pinned count toward total; concurrency probe (fake latencyMs 4 → peak in-flight prop.get = 4 exactly); estimateTokens vectors (empty/small/large rounding/unserializable); formatTokenEstimate vectors; timecodeLabel vectors (mm:ss.d, negative/NaN/Infinity → "00:00.0").
- Gates: pnpm --filter @studio/web typecheck GREEN (0 errors); test 124/124 GREEN (97 prior + 27 new); biome check scoped to src/lib/chat/{context,tokens}{,.test}.ts GREEN (2 organizeImports/format autofixes applied). Deviation noted: M4-SEAMS.md Seam-5 snippet shows opts {pinned?} without flags, but the M4-C brief pins opts {flags, pinned?} (and flags are what the popover/controller pass) — brief shape implemented, noted here per protocol. M4-D's git-sync sweep already carried mid-flight copies of my files in commits 80c642a/ce09e36; this commit lands the final state (biome fixes + the JSON.stringify-undefined fix).

Stage Summary:
- Deliverable: apps/web/src/lib/chat/context.ts + tokens.ts (+ colocated tests) — Seam 5 complete: deterministic bridge snapshot with user-controllable flags, per-block token estimates, full degradation ("missing context never breaks chat"), block.data treated as UNTRUSTED display+transport only.
- EXPORTED API for M4-B (chat-controller), M4-C2 (tool-executor), M4-H (context strip/popover), M4-G2 (footer estimate), M4-J (integration):
  - tokens.ts: `estimateTokens(value: unknown): number` (JSON length/4, floor 1 non-empty, 0 for null/undefined/"", never throws); `formatTokenEstimate(n: number): string` ("~420 tokens" / "~1.2k tokens").
  - context.ts: `interface ContextBlock { kind: keyof ContextSelection; label: string; tokenEstimate: number; data?: unknown }`; `interface SelectionBlockData { readonly layers: readonly (unknown|null)[]; readonly truncated?: boolean }`; `interface SelectedPropertiesBlockData { readonly layer: number; readonly properties: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly path: string; readonly value: unknown|null }> }`; `interface ContextSnapshot { flags: ContextSelection; blocks: ContextBlock[]; pinned: PinnedContextItem[]; totalTokenEstimate: number }`; `const DEFAULT_FLAGS: ContextSelection`; `function autoFlags(inspect: { readonly selectedLayers?: readonly number[] | null } | null): ContextSelection`; `function timecodeLabel(seconds: number | null | undefined): string` (mm:ss.d; "00:00.0" for nullish/negative/non-finite); `function commentsBlockPlaceholder(): ContextBlock`; `async function collectContextSnapshot(bridge: EditorBridge, opts: { flags: ContextSelection; pinned?: PinnedContextItem[] }): Promise<ContextSnapshot>` (never throws; off flags absent; selection-dependent flags absent without a selection; failed calls → " (unavailable)" + data undefined; pinned echoed + counted).
  - Block labels wave-2 renders: "Project summary", "Comp: <name>", "Selection: <names>", "Selected properties", "Playhead <mm:ss.d>", "Panel: <focused>", "Frame preview" (data-free placeholder — executor renders), "Recent commands", "Comments" (placeholder — gateway-fed, M4-J wires). Unavailable blocks get the " (unavailable)" suffix.
  - Query params M4-C2 must reuse (verified query.rs + fake.ts): project.summary {}; comp.info {comp?}; layer.tree {layer?, comp?, depth?, time?} (single-layer result; default layer = first selected); prop.get {layer?, path} (path required); edit.history.list {}. Playhead time from inspect is RAW seconds.
- Blockers: none. Notes for others: currentFrame chip data stays undefined by contract (renderPreview renders on demand); pinned chips are estimated with estimateTokens(item) — per-pinned-item estimates are NOT precomputed in the snapshot (call estimateTokens on the chip item in the UI).

- apps/web/src/lib/chat/tool-executor.ts (new): `executeClientTool(call, deps)` NEVER throws — order: isWriteTool → post `{error:"TOOL_FORBIDDEN", message:"Write tools are not available yet."}` (defensive; M4 registers none) → !isClientTool (plan_edit/server) → return WITHOUT posting (would 400 on unregistered id) → validateToolArgs fail → post `{error:"RUN_INVALID_TOOL", message:<zod>}` → execute under a 20s guard race (posts `{error:"TOOL_TIMEOUT", message:"The editor did not finish the tool call in time."}`; late results dropped via a `settled` flag, timer cleared on win) → post via `client.runs.postEvent(runId, {type:"tool.result", toolCallId, result}, {idempotencyKey:"toolresult:<runId>:<toolCallId>" .slice(0,128)})` — 3 attempts total (2 retries) on transient failures only (ApiError status 0/408/429/5xx; 4xx = permanent, no retry), then silent give-up (runner's waitForToolResult times out server-side — EDITOR_DISCONNECTED coupling, seam 2). `createToolExecutor(deps)` = closure + SYNCHRONOUS toolCallId set (never cleared → SSE-replayed tool.started is a no-op, never a re-run). get_context: ONE ui.inspect anchors 4 blocks; per-block `flagOrRemoved` — flag=true → data (null on bridge failure, per-item nulls never abort), flag=false → literal `"removed by user"`, block outside args.include → key ABSENT; blocks: projectSummary (project.summary), activeComp (comp.info {comp}), selection ({layerIds, tree: layer.tree {comp, layer, depth:2}}), playhead ({timeSeconds, playing}), focusedPanel, selectedProperties (prop.get ×6 curated paths for first selected layer — transform/{anchor,position,scale,rotation,opacity} + text/sourceText), recentCommands (edit.history.list → {current, recent: states.slice(-10)}). list_layers: comp.info {comp} → compact comp header + per-layer `layer.tree {comp, layer, depth}` (mapLimit 4; tree:null per-item on failure). get_properties: flat (layerId×path) pairs, mapLimit 4, per-item `{error:"NOT_FOUND"}` on missing layer/path; paths default to the 6 curated paths. render_preview: renderFrame {comp, time, max_side: min(maxWidth,1200)} → {comp, time, width, height, pngBase64|null}. Bridge failure mapping: DISCONNECT_CODES (EDITOR_DISCONNECTED/NOT_READY/LOAD_FAILED/TIMEOUT) → `EDITOR_DISCONNECTED`, else `TOOL_FAILED` (message via messageOf).
- SEAM DEVIATION (documented, per seam's own "verify param names BEFORE wiring"): seam 6 pins ONE call `bridge.query("layer.tree", {comp, depth})` for list_layers — verified reality is layer.tree is PER-LAYER and errors ("no `layer` given and no layer selected" / fake "select a layer first") when no selection exists, so the literal pin ships a list_layers that fails without a selection and lists nothing. The tool contract (§8.4 + contracts description: "layer ids, names and types… omit compId for the active composition") required comp-wide behavior: implemented as comp.info (authoritative layer list) + per-layer layer.tree at the asked depth, BOTH with the verified param names (comp/layer/depth). Covers all 8 pinned scenarios: (1) 4 tools happy paths incl. exact bridge params (comp {comp}, layer.tree {comp:1, layer:1, depth}, prop.get {layer:"1", path}, renderFrame {comp:"Main", time:1.5, max_side:800} → 1920×1080 scales to 800×450 / 1200×675) and the exact posted RUN_EVENT body (keys exactly [result, toolCallId, type]); (2) invalid args → RUN_INVALID_TOOL, zero bridge calls; (3) write tools → TOOL_FORBIDDEN, zero bridge calls; (4) plan_edit + garbage name → nothing posted, zero postEvent calls; (5) failOn layer.tree / prop.get mid-batch → per-item null/NOT_FOUND, still posts; (6) removed flags → "removed by user" + include-limiting → absent keys + no wasted bridge call; (7) duplicate toolCallId (concurrent + replayed) → single execution/post; (8) transient postEvent failures → exactly 3 attempts, same idempotency key each, success on 3rd; exhausted retries → silent give-up; 4xx → no retry; plus TOOL_TIMEOUT guard (hanging bridge) and no-double-post after the guard (late work dropped).

ChatController must NOT post tool.result itself for client tools (double-post would double-record the step + duplicate the SSE event).

ContextSelection/currentFrame/comments are not get_context blocks (currentFrame is render_preview's lazy capture; comments is M7) — include values referencing them are accepted by validation but produce no block. workspaceId is carried in deps for the M5 apply path; the events route derives the workspace from the session. Known server-side nuance (M4-K's side, not mine): a retry that succeeds after a lost response double-records the tool.result step (the route does not consume expected ids or honor Idempotency-Key) — my stable idempotency key is the client-side posture.

Work Log:
- Read the law first: worklog tail, M4-SEAMS Seam 4 (binding), AGENTS.md (import-type law), PA §7.4, ECUX §3.4/§8.1-8.4/§12, editor-session/types.ts header (the `import type` precedent), then the runtime wire: packages/api-client/src/{sse.ts,client.ts,api-error.ts,fixtures.ts} + gateway chat.ts replay. Verified apps/web vitest include covers src/** (colocated tests run by default).
- KEY SEAM FOUND (api-client sse.ts): streamChat ALREADY owns the whole §8.4 reconnect loop — it re-POSTs with the SAME body idempotencyKey + `Last-Event-ID: <normalizer.lastServerSeq>` on transport drops, backs off 1s/2s/4s/8s→30s max, pauses after 3 automatic attempts (onPause → controller parks RUN_PAUSED; `reconnect()` resumes, resetting the schedule), and ChatStreamController.cancel() IS the fused "POST /v1/runs/:id/cancel + abort the reader" (the brief's client.runs.cancel + abort in ONE call — a separate POST would double-cancel). The controller therefore CONSUMES onEvent/onError/onReconnect/onPause/onDone and never re-POSTs on its own. NOTE: the reader REMAPS seqs to strictly-increasing LOCAL seqs per streamChat lifecycle (server seqs are kept internally for the Last-Event-ID cursor) — the reducer's dedupe is per-run and cursor-reset on new-run takeover, so both wire shapes fold correctly.
- types.ts: RunPhase (M4 slice), ToolCardState (queued/running/done/failed + deltaText), ChatUiMessage, ChatUiError, ChatUiState exactly as pinned; plus the narrow runtime port ChatControllerClient + ChatStreamHandle (the EditorSessionClient pattern — real ApiClient satisfies it structurally, type-test-enforced) and ChatSendInput. contracts imported `import type` ONLY.
- run-state.ts: pure `foldEvent` — new-run takeover (run.started without an existing message creates the placeholder, flips streaming, clears stale error, RESETS the seq cursor per run), older-runs-frozen gate, seq<=lastSeq replay dedupe returning the SAME reference, terminal freeze for content events with terminal-event reconciliation (late run.completed after a local stop flips cancelled→done, §8.3), tool card upserts (result `{error: string; message?: string}` → failed card), plan/credits onto the message, preview/approval.required cursor-only (M5). applyUserMessage + applyClientAction (chat.submit/stop/reconnecting/paused/error) with total no-op guards; RUN_PAUSED_ERROR constant.
- chat-controller.ts: createChatController store (getSnapshot/subscribe/send/stop/retry/dispose + readonly runId). send: idempotencyKey = crypto.randomUUID() (≥8 chars), phase sending, steer queue (§3.4) while live — user message renders immediately, wire send flushes on terminal; HTTP envelope errors (INSUFFICIENT_CREDITS/MODEL_NOT_ENTITLED/RATE_LIMITED/…) map from the api-client's ApiError via onError → state.error (code/message/retryable); retry(): paused stream → stream.reconnect() (SAME run), pre-run failure → re-send SAME key (§8.4 never duplicates a run), terminal run failure → fresh key re-send without duplicating the user message; dispose aborts (never cancels) + freezes. getSnapshot referentially stable between changes (commit no-ops when foldEvent drops). onError callback fires on every error-state entry (fatal errors, run.failed, RUN_PAUSED; not stop()).

Stage Summary:
- Deliverable: apps/web/src/lib/chat/{types,run-state,chat-controller}.ts + 38 colocated tests — the wave-2 UI (M4-E..M4-J) can render the whole chat from ONE store with zero further engine work.
- The api-client SSE seam (found + documented, NOT a gap): reconnect/Last-Event-ID/backoff/pause/cancel all live in streamChat; web only maps callbacks → state. No api-client change request was needed (the brief anticipated a header hook; the reader sets Last-Event-ID itself from its server-seq cursor).
- Interface signatures for wave-2 (verbatim, apps/web/src/lib/chat):
  // types.ts
  export type RunPhase = "idle" | "sending" | "streaming" | "reconnecting" | "done" | "failed" | "cancelled";
  export interface ToolCardState { readonly toolCallId: string; readonly name: string; readonly args?: unknown; readonly status: "queued" | "running" | "done" | "failed"; readonly result?: unknown; readonly deltaText?: string; }
  export interface ChatUiMessage { readonly id: string; readonly role: "user" | "assistant"; readonly text: string; readonly runId?: string; readonly toolCards: readonly ToolCardState[]; readonly plan?: EditPlan; readonly credits?: { readonly reserved: number; readonly committed: number; readonly balance: number }; }
  export interface ChatUiError { readonly code: string; readonly message: string; readonly retryable: boolean; }
  export interface ChatUiState { readonly messages: readonly ChatUiMessage[]; readonly phase: RunPhase; readonly activeRunId: string | null; readonly error?: ChatUiError; readonly lastSeq: number; }
  export interface ChatStreamHandle { readonly paused: boolean; cancel(reason?: string): Promise<void>; abort(): void; reconnect(): Promise<void>; }
  export interface ChatControllerClient { readonly chat: { stream(threadId: string, body: ChatRequest, handlers?: ChatStreamHandlers & { signal?: AbortSignal; reconnect?: ChatStreamReconnectConfig }): ChatStreamHandle; }; }   // ApiClient satisfies it structurally
  export interface ChatSendInput { readonly message: string; readonly context: ContextSelection; readonly pinned: PinnedContextItem[]; readonly model?: ModelId; }
  // run-state.ts
  export function createInitialChatUiState(): ChatUiState;
  export const RUN_PAUSED_ERROR: ChatUiError;   // { code: "RUN_PAUSED", message: "Run paused. Check your connection.", retryable: true }
  export type ChatClientAction = { type: "chat.submit" } | { type: "chat.stop" } | { type: "chat.reconnecting" } | { type: "chat.paused" } | { type: "chat.error"; error: ChatUiError };
  export function applyUserMessage(state: ChatUiState, message: { id: string; text: string }): ChatUiState;
  export function applyClientAction(state: ChatUiState, action: ChatClientAction): ChatUiState;
  export function foldEvent(state: ChatUiState, event: StudioStreamEvent): ChatUiState;   // pure, total, replay-idempotent; dropped events return the SAME reference
  export const TERMINAL_RUN_PHASES: ReadonlySet<RunPhase>;  // done|failed|cancelled
  export const LIVE_RUN_PHASES: ReadonlySet<RunPhase>;      // sending|streaming|reconnecting
  // chat-controller.ts
  export interface CreateChatControllerOptions { readonly client: ChatControllerClient; readonly workspaceId: string; readonly projectId: string; readonly threadId: string; readonly onError?: (error: { code: string; message: string }) => void; }
  export interface ChatController { readonly runId: string | null; getSnapshot(): ChatUiState; subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void; send(input: ChatSendInput): void; stop(): void; retry(): void; dispose(): void; }
  export function createChatController(options: CreateChatControllerOptions): ChatController;
- Wave-2 wiring notes: mount with useSyncExternalStore(controller.subscribe, controller.getSnapshot); tool-executor observation = diff toolCards status "running" on the active runId message (or subscribe + snapshot diff — no event emitter by design, pinned surface only); queued steer messages render as user messages immediately (the queue itself is controller-internal, NOT in UiState); phase "reconnecting" persists until the next ACCEPTED event (the api-client has no "reconnected-OK" callback — banner until the stream proves liveness or settles); workspaceId/projectId are accepted + held for the M4-C2/M4-J wiring seams (not used on any wire the engine touches).

- context-strip.tsx ('use client'): pinned ContextStripProps verbatim + the brief's optional `pinned?: readonly PinnedContextItem[]` extension → ContextStrip: ReactElement | null. Renders null when blocks empty AND pinned empty/absent (hooks-before-null order is rules-of-hooks-safe). Expanded form = a single-line ul (aria-label "Chat context, ~N tokens total" — totalTokenEstimate surfaces through the accessible name; per-block estimates live in the popover per §5.3): one removable chip per ACTIVE block (`[✓ label]` + full-height ✕ → onRemoveBlock(kind), aria-label "Remove <label> from context"), pinned chips with kind glyphs (frame ▣, selection ◉, composition ▤, asset ⬡, comment ✎ — display-only in the strip per the PIN: pinned removal lives in the popover), trailing "+" button → onAddContext (aria-label "Add context", @studio/ui Tooltip "Add context to this message"). h-8 (32px), overflow-x-auto, whitespace-nowrap chips, scrollbar-thin, tokens-only classes.
- Narrow-collapse contract (§3.2): a ResizeObserver on the strip element itself (container width, not viewport) collapses below 480px to a "Context N" summary chip (N = blocks + pinned) that routes to onAddContext (picker stays reachable when chips are hidden) and carries title "~N tokens total". Unknown width (no measurement yet / no ResizeObserver — the jsdom stub case) keeps the expanded form: deterministic in tests, correct in browsers (observer fires on mount). Strict < 480 (480 exactly = expanded). Tests drive the branch with a width-firing ResizeObserver mock, restored after each test.
- context-popover.tsx ('use client'): pinned ContextPopoverProps verbatim + the brief's optional `pinned?: readonly PinnedContextItem[]` and `onRemovePinned?(index: number)` extensions → ContextPopover. Radix modal popover (@studio/ui Popover + PopoverAnchor + PopoverContent): always-open controlled Root, dismissal (Esc / outside-click) maps onOpenChange(false) → onDone. The "overlay" is Radix's modal outside-pointer-events layer: any pointerdown+click outside the card dismisses — no painted scrim (§10 no-glass; Cursor-style light dismissal), body gets pointer-events:none while open, background aria-hidden'd (hideOthers), body scroll locked. Focus trap = Radix FocusScope (trapped+loop): first focusable row gets initial focus, Tab/Shift+Tab wrap inside (asserted in tests), focus never escapes.
- Popover content per §5.3 mock: title "Context for this message"; one row per passed block = NATIVE input[type=checkbox] (accent-signal — biome a11y useSemanticElements wanted semantic elements; the repo has no checkbox primitive and the command-palette suppression precedent was avoidable here) + truncated label (title tooltip) + estimate column via formatTokenEstimate(block.tokenEstimate); currentFrame with data===undefined shows "1 image" instead of tokens (lazy render block, M4-C contract); optional pinned section = Badge "Pinned" + signal-tinted chips with glyphs + ✕ per chip → onRemovePinned(index), display-only when the callback is omitted, absent when the list is empty; empty blocks → "No context will be sent."; footer = "~N tokens total" (data-testid context-total) + [Remove all] (secondary, optimistic uncheck of every row) + [Done] (primary) — both @studio/ui Button sm (28px §7.1). Rows list max-h-72 overflow-y-auto scrollbar-thin. NEVER renders block.data (labels/estimates only — asserted with a sentinel string in tests); role="dialog" comes from Radix PopoverContent natively (my explicit role attr removed to satisfy biome useSemanticElements — the rendered element still has role="dialog" + aria-label="Chat context", asserted via getByRole).
- Toggle semantics (the on→off→on contract): the popover renders one row per passed block, all checked (the parent passes active blocks — trust props on the strip; the popover keeps an OPTIMISTIC pending-uncheck overlay so on↔off is instant and reversible within one open popover: onToggleBlock(kind, on) fires immediately, the row visibly unchecks, and pending unchecks are pruned against the incoming blocks on every props change — parent state always wins once it lands; re-added blocks come back checked). [Remove all] optimistically unchecks every current row + fires onRemoveAll() once.
- Esc ordering (§9): PopoverContent onEscapeKeyDown stopPropagation — this Esc closes the popover only; a panel-level Esc listener never sees it, so the panel never collapses on the same keystroke. Mounting contract for M4-J documented in the file header: render as a child of the chat panel's positioned root next to the strip; the anchor span pins the card to panel top-right (below the 48px header, 12px inset, align="end", sideOffset 8) and Radix portals + collision-flips from there.
- Tests (2 files, colocated): 26 tests / 60+ expect() assertions, deterministic (real timers, no network, ~1.5s test time; Radix modal machinery exercised through the same path as packages/ui dialog tests). Popover (14): role dialog + name "Chat context" + all four row labels + all four estimate strings ("~420 tokens"…"~1.2k tokens"); frame row "1 image" with NO "tokens" text; toggle on→off→on with exact call args ([kind,false] then [kind,true]) and checked-state flips; Remove all unchecks every row + fires once; Done fires onDone; total line "~1.9k tokens total"; Esc → onDone (user.keyboard "{Escape}"); outside pointerdown+click → onDone (Radix's deferred outside-dismissal awaited); focus trap (initial focus inside, Shift+Tab wraps first→Done, Tab wraps Done→first, 8×Tab never escapes); pinned chips with glyphs + ✕ removal by index with accessible name; pinned display-only without the callback; no pinned section when absent/empty; block.data sentinel never appears in the DOM; empty-context note + "~0 tokens total".

- Narrow-collapse contract for M4-J: the strip measures ITSELF via ResizeObserver (contentRect.width of its own ul) — mount it in a container that actually tracks the panel width (a slot div works); <480px renders the "Context N" summary button (N = blocks + pinned, fires onAddContext, title "~N tokens total"); the popover anchor span is absolute top-12 right-3 inside the popover's own tree — mount ContextPopover as a direct child of the panel's positioned root, near the strip; Radix portals the card to body and collision-flips from the anchor (no manual positioning needed, but the panel root must be `position: relative/absolute` for the anchor to sit under the header).
- Wiring facts for M4-J: pass snapshot.blocks as blocks (active only — removed blocks are simply absent from props; the popover's optimistic pending-uncheck prunes itself against incoming blocks so stale unchecks never leak); totalTokenEstimate = snapshot.totalTokenEstimate; onToggleBlock(kind,on) maps straight onto the flags object (collectContextSnapshot re-collects with the new flags; the executor reports user-removed flags as "removed by user" — M4-C2 already honors it); onRemoveAll clears every present block flag; onAddContext/onDone both toggle your popover-open state (Done/Esc/outside-click all funnel through onDone — one dismissal path); Esc is stopPropagation'd by the popover so your panel Esc handler must expect the popover to have consumed it while open (§9 popovers-before-panel). Focus after dismissal: Radix modal has no trigger to restore to — refocus your "Context N" header button in onDone if you want the keyboard trail preserved. currentFrame rows show "1 image" only while block.data === undefined (always, per the collector); pinned chip per-item token estimates are NOT in the snapshot — call estimateTokens(item) if you ever need them (M4-C note, unchanged).

Blockers: none.

- QUIRK VERDICT (M4-A's double-step-row flag — CONFIRMED with live DB evidence): one client tool call ⇒ THREE agent_steps rows: seq 0 status success with the result (runs.ts sink, seq = steps.length at POST time), seq 1 status running with args (chat.ts finalize recordSteps from the log seq), seq 2 status success with the result again (finalize). (run_id, seq) UNIQUE prevents exact-duplicate seqs in the happy path; replays collide and recordSteps swallows. Cancelled runs keep an orphaned running row. Assertion kept tolerant per the brief (≥2 rows, all the tool, unique seqs, args row present, result row present); suggested one-owner fix noted in agent-ctx, NOT applied (law #12).
- SRC/ BUGS REPORTED (never fixed — law #12): (1) LEDGER SETTLE DOUBLE-DEDUCTION: planSettlement refunds `outstanding − commit` while commit −M deducts on top of the hold → a completed run nets −2×commit (grant 100 → reserve 2 → commit 1 → refund 1 → balance 98, doc-correct 99 per §8.8). The @studio/db integration test "ledger: grant → reserve → settle" already pins the doc-correct value (82) and observes 64 — RED whenever DATABASE_URL is a postgres URL (the db package's integration suite was evidently never gated with postgres on this box). My test pins the observed entry arithmetic with a loud LEDGER BUG PIN comment. (2) Step-status vocabulary mismatch: writers emit running/success; runs.ts toRecord maps only started/succeeded/failed → every chat-loop step surfaces as "skipped" in GET /v1/runs/:id (code-verified). (3) run.completed is emitted BEFORE the run-row update/assistant insert/step rows (finalize ordering) — DB readers right after the terminal frame see stale rows; my harness polls. (4) ABORT SEMANTICS PIN: a mid-run client disconnect CANCELS the live run (chat.ts wires the hono request-abort signal AND the SSE ReadableStream cancel to cancelRun(); @hono/node-server 2.1.5 aborts the request signal on premature close) → refund + run.cancelled; the api-client's §8.4 reconnect loop can therefore only replay TERMINAL logs — "live run survives a network flap" is NOT what the gateway implements. M4 closeout should rule whether disconnect-cancels is intended (test 3b pins it as actual behavior; test 3a covers the live-parallel replay).

- Surveyed all 78 registration files; hand-located every seed id; wrote packages/contracts/scripts/extract-engine-registry.mjs (NEW): depth- and string-aware scanner that handles MULTI-LINE registrations (layer.rs:1592 layer.newText et al.), keeps only string-literal first args (macro-def bodies like `$crate::cmd!($id,…)` and `style_cmd!`/`view_cmd!` wrappers auto-skip), reads the pin from editor/UPSTREAM.md, and is idempotent (extractedAt preserved on unchanged content → byte-stable fixture).
- Ran it: fixtures/engine-command-ids.json = {extractedAt, enginePin 9db7f3b21700fd821f215782ed1c82afe077880f, ids: 580 sorted cmd! ids, queries: 63 sorted query! ids}. Counts vs SEAMS' 558+52 explained: the planning numbers came from a single-line grep (naive `query!\("` finds exactly 52; multi-line registrations are invisible to it); the multiline-aware scan finds the rest — 0 duplicates, uniqueness/sortedness/cross-kind disjointness verified, spot-checked ids against source lines.
- Replaced the command-registry.ts stub: 37 curated ids = the 26 SEAMS seed ids + 11 clearly-safe writes (layer.setText, layer.style.add/remove/toggle, layer.applyTextPreset, text.delete, prop.toggleKey, prop.reset, keys.set, effect.toggle, effect.reset). Every entry: verbatim Rust title + params doc (file:line pinned per entry) parsed into CommandParamInfo[] by a parser that mirrors the engine's grammar exactly: pieces split at top-level `,`; `|` before the first `:` = alternative NAMES (path|prop → two OPTIONAL descriptors, engine parity); `|` after `:` = value hint (mode?: Normal|Multiply|…); `name?` optional / bare name required; `{}` = no params; nameless `|` fragments (#hex + [r,g,b]) fold into the hint; parentheticals = param descriptions; prose after `}` (— / → / (…)) = command description.
- Kept the pinned exports (AGENT_COMMAND_REGISTRY / AGENT_COMMAND_IDS / isAgentCommand — barrel re-exports via export *) and added AGENT_COMMAND_UNDOABLE (ReadonlySet; batch-level heuristic ONLY: batch.rs fold() merges a batch's undo steps into ONE step labeled with the batch label, edit.undo/edit.redo are real — per-command undo steps deliberately NOT claimed) and META_COMMAND_IDS (["command.describe","command.list","engine.batch"] — transport/meta, never batchable; engine.batch is a raw CommandSpec in batch.rs and the engine's NOT_IN_BATCH list rejects it inside batches).
- tests/command-registry.test.ts (NEW, 36 tests): curated ⊆ fixture (provenance), fixture freshness vs live UPSTREAM.md pin + count band + sorted/unique, SEAMS seed coverage + 25-40 band, id pattern [a-z][a-zA-Z0-9.]* + __proto__/constructor/toString rejection, well-formedness (id===key, unique params, typed), isAgentCommand boundaries (case/prefix/whitespace/transport ids), META disjointness, UNDOABLE semantics, checkBatchCommands integration against the REAL registry, params arrays hand-verified against the Rust source lines for ~20 commands (comp.open, layer.newSolid, keys.move, keys.set, text.addSelector, prop.addKey, layer.arrange, layer.newText, layer.setText, comp.new, prop.set, layer.setBlendMode, anim.applyPreset, anim.applyBuiltinPreset, effect.*, layer.style.*, layer.applyTextPreset, text.insert/delete, edit.undo/redo), and extraction-script determinism (spawned twice from the package dir → identical fixture hash, status "unchanged").
- Gates: pnpm --filter @studio/contracts typecheck → 0 errors; test → 187/187 green (36 mine); biome check --write on all three code files → clean. git-sync first attempt committed locally but failed to push (6 retries); re-ran the sanctioned script → OK (1693dfc + 76c7fb2 on origin/main).
Stage Summary:
- Files: packages/contracts/scripts/extract-engine-registry.mjs (NEW), packages/contracts/fixtures/engine-command-ids.json (NEW, committed), packages/contracts/src/command-registry.ts (stub→full), packages/contracts/tests/command-registry.test.ts (NEW). Barrel untouched (export * pre-wired); engine-batch.ts/write-tools.ts untouched.
- Decisions/notes for the wave: (1) fixture scope = cmd!/query! registrations in commands/*.rs per the seam; the live engine also registers ~26 ids in models/templates/storage/preview/learn .rs (roto/face model install/download/remove, template file ops, storage.clear — file-system/destructive, never agent-batchable) and ~59 viewer/frontend ids via local fe!/res!/fast!/chan! wrappers, plus engine.batch as a raw CommandSpec — all outside the fixture and none curated (documented in the script header). (2) Engine parity on requiredness: alternatives (path|prop, path | preset) are OPTIONAL — so prop.reset/anim.applyPreset have no required params, exactly like the engine's params_schema. (3) mod.rs ALWAYS_OK lets every command take `comp`/`merge` params even when undocumented — the plan compiler must not flag steps carrying them (my registry mirrors the docs, and checkBatchCommands only checks required presence, so no false negatives). (4) prop.setExpression deliberately NOT curated (arbitrary expression code — wants a DECISIONS.md ruling before any widening). (5) Registry regeneration after an engine re-pin = re-run the script + commit; the freshness test fails loudly until then.

High risk ⇒ border-danger + "High risk — requires confirmation" line + explicit confirm CHECKBOX gating Apply (disabled until checked) per needsConfirmCheckbox; checked Apply then opens ChatConfirmDialog ("Apply this high-risk plan?", danger) before onApply fires — low/medium Apply emits directly. Steps: title + tool id behind a disclosure toggle (aria-expanded/aria-controls, default EXPANDED so the M4-L integration assertions on plan-steps keep passing). Affected layers: name + change rows, defensively capped at the §8.6 bound of 50 (+ "+N more layers not shown"). Credits: displays the estimatedCredits prop (server-recomputed plan-event value) VERBATIM, falling back to plan.estimatedCredits — zero client credit math (proven by test: shows 12, not the 2 the formula would give). Preview: optional `preview?: ReactNode` slot rendered ABOVE the action row (M5-H's PlanPreview injects via the thread layer — never imported here, law #12). Long summaries/names/step titles truncate (`truncate` + min-w-0 + title attr) and carry dir="auto" so hostile/RTL text renders as DATA without flipping card layout. M4 note prop kept but the pinned "Apply arrives with M5 — this plan is a preview." default is GONE (apply is real). Status transitions away from "proposed" reset the checkbox + close the dialog (no stale gates).
- States (data-status stamped on the card): proposed (Apply/Cancel; Cancel = explicit onCancel, run stays usable), applying (Spinner + "Applying…", no buttons), applied ("Applied — Version <label> · one undo step." + Undo→onUndo), undone ("…version stays in history." + Re-apply→onReapply), undone+reapplyDrifted (Re-apply disabled + "Re-apply is blocked — the project moved on since this run was undone. Ask the agent to plan the edit again."), failed ("The edit didn't apply — nothing changed." + statusDetail + Try again→onApply), conflict (TriangleAlert + "Apply was rejected — the project moved on…" + "Re-apply is blocked for this plan…" — NO actions, the 409 explanation), ambiguous-resolution (candidates from resolutionRequests; buttons emit onResolveCandidate(requestId, candidate), inert spans when no callback), cancelled ("Plan cancelled — nothing was applied. The chat stays ready.").
- M4 backward compatibility: `applied?: boolean` widens the pinned `applied: false` (message.tsx's `<PlanCard plan={…} applied={false} />` still typechecks; true resolves to status applied); a callback-less mount renders NO Apply/Cancel/checkbox (read-only stays read-only — M4-L's "no apply control" proof still passes). No role="status" live regions inside the card: the thread scroller IS the aria-live region (thread.tsx §9.2) — nested live regions would double-announce, and biome's useSemanticElements <output> suggestion would duplicate the Spinner's own <output> (documented in-file).

Task ID: M5-K
Agent: M5-K (gateway apply-flow E2E — M5 exit-criteria proof)
Task: The M5 EXIT-CRITERIA PROOF at the gateway level — the FULL loop E2E (real buildApp + real Postgres + real LiteLLM runner vs an in-process fake OpenAI SSE server on the real SSE wire): model proposes a write plan via studio.plan_edit → server-side compile → plan event with compiledBatch + resolutionRequests → client applies (version uploaded through the real versions route with agentRunId) → ONE version, ONE commit → undo → reapply gate.
Work Log:
- Read the mandatory set in order: AGENTS.md (law), worklog tail (M4-K's chat-loop harness + pins/quirks, M5-prep/D/J/G, M5-wave1-integration), M5-SEAMS.md + BUILD_PLAN §M5 exit criteria + DECISIONS D-6/D-19/D-20, then the surfaces: gateway tests/chat-loop.test.ts (harness copied, never imported), routes/{apply,chat,runs,versions}.ts, plan-compiler.ts, runner.ts, litellm/runner.ts (RUNNER_WRITE_TOOLS + WRITE_TOOL_RESULT_SCHEMA + the plan_edit/write-tool paths), contracts write-tools/command-registry/engine-batch/edit-plan/events/routes-apply, db repos runs/versions/ledger + ledger-math, tests/helpers.ts, M5-E's apply.test.ts (route contract).
- Built services/gateway/tests/apply-flow.test.ts (ONLY owned path; NEW, ~1240 lines): duplicated FakeOpenAIServer + hand-rolled SseStream + expectValidStream per M4-K's precedent; bootServer({MOCK_MODEL:false, LITELLM_BASE_URL:fake.url, CHAT_MAX_STEPS:6}) + createRunnerForEnv — the production wiring. Scripted rounds: studio.plan_edit {goal} → strict-JSON EditPlan (model LIES: risk "high", estimatedCredits 99 — server recomputes "low"/2) → final answer; the plan = two studio.create_text steps → compiled to TWO registry layer.newText commands, affectedLayers Headline+Sub → resolutionRequests lr-1/lr-2 (drives the REAL compile through the real route: AGENT_COMMAND_REGISTRY + WRITE_TOOL_ARGS_SCHEMAS via defaultCompileDeps).
- 12 scenarios (test names are the evidence): (1) happy apply loop — plan event compiledBatch {label "Agent: Add two text layers", atomic:true, 2×layer.newText} + lr-1/lr-2 + recomputed risk/credits; apply → 200 {applied, versionId, creditsCommitted:0}; run row applied (applied_at/batch sha/version per M5-D, resultVersionId backfilled, status untouched); ledger reserve −2/commit −1/refund +2 (exactly ONE commit, run nets −1); exactly ONE version with agentRunId; agent_steps 2 rows (single-writer pin). (2) idempotency: same key → middleware replay byte-equal + x-idempotent-replay; fresh key + same logical apply → route replay branch, 200; no new version/commit. (3) double-apply, fresh key + new sha → 409 RUN_ALREADY_APPLIED, recorded apply untouched. (4) version conflict: head moves (v3 uploaded) before the first apply → 409 VERSION_CONFLICT "The project changed while this plan was open.", no partial state. (5) guard-order pin: reapply of an APPLIED run after head drift → 409 RUN_ALREADY_APPLIED (lifecycle guard fires BEFORE the §9.3 lineage checks; one apply per run row). (6) undo: 200, run undone (undoneAt/undoneBy, apply record stays), version row SURVIVES immutable (§9.3: same sha, same agentRunId, count 2), ledger untouched (undo is free), reapply gate 409. (7) undo before apply → 409 RUN_NOT_APPLIED; undo replay same key (middleware) AND fresh key (route state idempotency) → 200 with undoneAt unchanged. (8) cross-tenant apply/undo/GET → 404 RUN_NOT_FOUND everywhere, never 403. (9) unknown run → 404; malformed body / uppercase / short / garbage sha → 400 VALIDATION_FAILED; state + balance unchanged. (10) insufficient credits: drained to 0 via ledger.adjust → 402 INSUFFICIENT_CREDITS BEFORE any state change (no applied columns, no new ledger rows, balance 0, version intact); top-up → retry 200 (no poisoned state). (11) safety pin: compiledBatch ids ⊆ AGENT_COMMAND_IDS + isAgentCommand, atomic, "Agent: <summary>"; provider wire = exactly the 13 sorted ids (5 read + 8 write); the server-side plan round is TOOLS-FREE; the model-facing tool result is the BARE plan (no compiledBatch/resolutionRequests ever enter the model conversation). (12) write-tool propose path live: create_text → tool.result = WRITE_TOOL_RESULT_SCHEMA envelope {proposedBatch, note} + model fed {proposed, summary, note, stepCount} (no command ids — law #5); NO plan event for write-tool proposals.
- CONTRACT DRIFT PINNED #1 (M5-A ⇄ M5-B args vocabulary, law #12, report-only, pinned at the live wire in test 12): plan-compiler.ts translate() reads the M5-B fixture vocabulary (set_properties `updates:[{layer,path,value}]`, set_keyframes `layer`+`keyframes`, add_effect `layer`+`effect`, apply_preset `targets`+`preset`) while the M5-A contracts schemas the live runner validates against speak `layers:[{name}]`+`properties|keys|effect:{name}|preset:{name|builtinId}`. A studio.set_properties call with REAL M5-A args passes args validation but compiles to ZERO commands → fatal empty_batch → the tool answers {error:"PROPOSAL_INVALID", message:"the plan compiles to an empty command batch (only annotation steps)"} (run survives). EVERY layer-referencing write tool (set_properties/add_effect/set_keyframes/apply_preset) is currently un-compilable through the live seam; create_composition/create_text/undo_run compile fine (my plan uses create_text for exactly this reason — the brief's set_properties example cannot compile through the real route today). Owner: M5-B plan-compiler.ts translate() must accept the M5-A contracts shapes (or a DECISIONS ruling). Suggested fix shape: translate set_properties via args.layers×args.properties, set_keyframes via args.layers×args.keys({timeSeconds→time}), add_effect via args.layers+args.effect.name, apply_preset via args.layers/preset.
- CONTRACT DRIFT PINNED #2 (D-20 in the live loop, test 1): the chat finalize settles the run's whole hold at run.completed (reserve −N, commit −M usage, refund +N per D-20), so the apply's planSettlement sees outstanding 0 → creditsCommitted 0 in the FULL loop; the run nets exactly its single usage commit. The route documents this ("never a double charge"); the net = −planCredits reading is the seeded-hold world M5-E's apply.test.ts proves. My E2E pins the live-loop side: exactly ONE commit entry, no apply-time deduction, no apply:* settle entries.
- CONTRACT DRIFT PINNED #3 (route guard order vs BP M5.5, tests 4+5): VERSION_CONFLICT is only reachable BEFORE the first apply (proposed runs whose head drifted); an already-applied run answers RUN_ALREADY_APPLIED regardless of drift. - FINDINGS (report-only, law #12 — no src/ files touched):
  - F-L1 (IMPORTANT — the saveNow-after-batch divergence, task-mandated judgment): the controller handles it HONESTLY (outcome message says the edit "is in the editor only"; no applyRun — correct per §M5.4's order: version first, then report+commit), BUT the recovery path is dangerous: the plan card's failed-state "Try again" is a full re-apply that re-executes engine.batch onto the already-applied engine state — pinned proof: a non-idempotent plan DOUBLES locally (3 layers, 2 identically-labeled undo entries) and then reports success. Recommendation: for reasons save-failed/save-conflict the card should offer "retry the save" (saveNow + applyRun ONLY — the engine step is not in question), not a blind re-apply; alternatively the controller can persist-and-report on retry without re-executing.
  - F-L2 (idempotency-key reuse gap vs the brief): apply.ts mints crypto.randomUUID() per gesture (apply.ts:559) and retains NOTHING — the brief's expected "client reuses the same key on the retry" does NOT exist as-landed (pinned: fresh key per retry gesture; exactly one POST per gesture, so no client-side double-POST). The safety net is M5-K's gateway replay branch ({batchSha256, versionId} content addressing + middleware key replay) — which my tests show HOLDS while the autosave dedupes identical content (same pair) and MISSES after a re-executed non-idempotent batch (same sha, NEW versionId → 409 RUN_ALREADY_APPLIED or a fresh commit if the original never landed). Suggested fix: retain the gesture key per runId until a terminal success/conflict (the tool-executor.ts:501 toolresult-key precedent) — one map, no wire change.
  - F-L3 (seam, for the integrator): M5-G's plan-card failed state renders "The edit didn't apply — nothing changed." + statusDetail — for save-failed the statusDetail says the edit WAS applied locally: the two lines contradict. The thread layer (not yet wired) needs a distinct status for save-failed/save-conflict (e.g. a "saved locally" state whose action is retry-the-save, not re-apply) or must suppress the default line.
  - F-L4 (stack-blind undo): controller.undo(runId) runs edit.undo + undoRun(runId) without checking WHICH undo entry the engine popped — pinned: after applying run_1 then run_2, undo(run_1) reverts run_2's batch (opacity back to run_1's value) while the gateway records run_1 as the undone run. Fix direction: compare the engine's {undone: label} answer against the plan's batch label before undoRun (fail typed on mismatch), or serialize undo gestures LIFO at the card layer.
  - F-L5 (LOW, fake-only, code-verified): packages/editor-bridge/src/fake.ts cmdBatch resets suppressedJournals ONLY inside fold() (~line 817) — the atomic-failure path (restore without fold) leaks the counter, so a subsequent ZERO-journal batch (queries-only steps) folds a spurious undo entry + dirty flip. Upstream keeps the counter per-batch (Rust local), so this is fake-fidelity only; harmless to every current suite (each failed-batch test asserts immediately).
  - F-L6 (LOW): apply.ts's seam is bridge.execute("engine.batch", …) which bypasses normalizeAgentBatch (§8.5 bounds live only on bridge.batch()) and its own validCompiledBatch is shape-only — pinned: a 150-step batch and a 100k-char label execute through the engine unbounded. Mitigation in place: the plan event's compiledBatch is AGENT_BATCH_SCHEMA-validated on the SSE wire (contracts events.ts:90), so server-fed plans are pre-bounded; a future in-memory plan source would not be. - PINNED SIGNATURES for M6-C / M5-O (consume EXACTLY these; typed DbError throws, workspaceId positional first, cross-tenant = NOT_FOUND/null):
  - repos.subscriptions.getByOrganization(workspaceId): Promise<Subscription | null>
  - repos.subscriptions.upsertState(workspaceId, { planId: PlanId; status: SubscriptionStatus; stripeSubscriptionId?: string|null; currentPeriodEnd?: Date|null; trialEnd?: Date|null; cancelAtPeriodEnd?: boolean; graceEnd?: Date|null }): Promise<Subscription> — atomic INSERT … ON CONFLICT (organization_id) DO UPDATE (one row per workspace, race-proof)
  - repos.subscriptions.transitionStatus(workspaceId, { from: readonly SubscriptionStatus[]; to: SubscriptionStatus; planId?; currentPeriodEnd?; trialEnd?; cancelAtPeriodEnd?; graceEnd? }): Promise<Subscription> — GUARDED conditional update: status re-checked in the UPDATE WHERE (inArray), so two concurrent webhook transitions of the same state can NEVER both apply; loser gets typed CONFLICT carrying the actual current status. Missing workspace subscription → NOT_FOUND. transitionStatus re-pins any provided optional fields (webhook renewal/past-due events).
  - repos.subscriptions.findStripeCustomer(workspaceId): Promise<StripeCustomer | null>
  - repos.subscriptions.ensureStripeCustomer(workspaceId, { stripeCustomerId: string; cardFingerprint?: string|null }): Promise<StripeCustomer> — upsert on organization_id; fingerprint ONLY written when a non-empty string is given (never cleared), new card overwrites
  - repos.subscriptions.cardFingerprintUsed(fingerprint: string): Promise<{ organizationId: string } | null> — one-trial-per-card abuse rule (BP M6.5); deliberately NOT tenant-scoped (cross-workspace by design), returns one workspace holding the fingerprint (any match answers the check)
  - repos.invoices.record(workspaceId, { stripeInvoiceId; amountCents: number; currency: string; status: InvoiceStatus; periodStart?; periodEnd?; hostedUrl?; pdfUrl? }): Promise<{ invoice: Invoice; replayed: boolean }> — idempotent on stripeInvoiceId: re-record returns the SAME row (mutable fields updated in place — draft→paid progression from later webhooks is the point), replayed=true iff the row already existed; same stripeInvoiceId under ANOTHER workspace = typed CONFLICT (ledger findExisting precedent); concurrent first-records settle via the UNIQUE index (loser re-reads the winner's row) — never a duplicate

  - repos.settings.getUserAutoApply(userId): Promise<boolean> — false when the user_settings row is absent (the default)
  - repos.settings.setUserAutoApply(userId, enabled: boolean): Promise<UserSettings> — upsert on user_id PK; unknown user → NOT_FOUND
  - repos.webhookEvents.seenOrRecord(provider, eventId, { type?, payload? }?): Promise<{ seen: boolean }> — {seen:false} exactly once per (provider, eventId) incl. racing deliveries (UNIQUE index arbitrates, 23505 → seen); payload kept as payload_json for reconciliation (BP M6.3)

Task ID: M5-O
Agent: general-purpose subagent (M5-O)
Task: BUILD_PLAN §M5 task 6 — the AUTO-APPLY policy: a PURE server-side decision module (off by default, low-risk reversible edits only), the additive plan-event wiring in routes/chat.ts through the pinned M6-D settings seam, the optional typed PLAN_EVENT_SCHEMA.autoApply field, and the full decision-matrix + wiring test suite.
Work Log:
- Read the mandatory set in order: AGENTS.md (laws #1/#4/#5/#12), worklog tail (M5-K/M/L/M/N + M5-wave1-integration closeout), BUILD_PLAN §M5 task 6 + §9 scope-cut order (auto-apply is cut #1, ships OFF), PRODUCT_ARCHITECTURE §7.3 envelope, EDITOR_CHAT_UX §7.3 (auto-apply semantics: off by default; only low-risk reversible; never delete/render/footage/destructive/credit-above-threshold; per-user setting stored server-side) + §6.2/§7.1 context, contracts edit-plan.ts (AUTO_APPLYABLE_INTENTS/isAutoApplicable: edit|style|animate), engine-batch.ts (AgentBatch/BatchStep), plan-compiler.ts (CompilePlanResult; risk/estimatedCredits recomputed server-side), routes/chat.ts (the streamApi.emit seam — every runner-emitted event funnels through the route), runner.ts (RunStreamPayload/MockRunner/mockEditPlan), litellm/runner.ts (the compile-enriched plan event), command-registry.ts (AGENT_COMMAND_IDS), helpers.ts (bootServer/chat-loop harness), M5-K/M5-N/M5-M test precedents, and M6-D's pinned seam from the task brief.
- services/gateway/src/auto-apply.ts (NEW, pure — no IO, no clocks, no randomness): `autoApplyDecision({plan, compiled: CompilePlanResult, userAutoApplyEnabled: boolean}): AutoApplyDecision` = `{mode:"auto", reason:"eligible"} | {mode:"propose", reason}`. DECISION MATRIX, checked in this order (first failure is the reason): (1) user setting enabled (default false) → "setting-disabled"; (2) compiled.ok → "compile-failed"; (3) SERVER-recomputed compiled.risk === "low" → "risk-not-low"; (4) !plan.requiresApproval → "approval-required"; (5) plan.intent ∈ AUTO_APPLYABLE_INTENTS (contracts: edit|style|animate; create/organize are structural, render is spend-heavy export, clarify/explain change nothing) → "intent-not-auto-applyable"; (6) EVERY compiled batch command ∈ REVERSIBLE_AUTO_COMMANDS → "command-not-reversible"; (7) server-recomputed estimatedCredits finite, ≥ 0, ≤ AUTO_APPLY_MAX_CREDITS → "credits-above-ceiling" (NaN/Infinity/negative/non-number all fail closed); (8) compiled batch step count 1..AUTO_APPLY_MAX_STEPS → "too-many-steps" (a malformed compiled-ok-but-ZERO-step result also lands here — fail-closed, the compiler's own contract is ≥ 1). Exports: AUTO_APPLY_MAX_STEPS=10, AUTO_APPLY_MAX_CREDITS=3, REVERSIBLE_AUTO_COMMANDS (frozen ReadonlySet DERIVED from AGENT_COMMAND_IDS presence — an id that ever leaves the curated registry drops out fail-closed), AUTO_APPLY_SETTING_UNAVAILABLE (frozen propose fallback), AUTO_APPLY_REASON_MESSAGES + describeAutoApplyReason (law-#1-safe human copy), the AutoApplyDecision/AutoApplyReason types.
- REVERSIBLE subset (hardcoded 9, registry-provenance-pinned by test): prop.set, layer.rename, text.insert, layer.newText, prop.addKey, anim.applyPreset, anim.applyBuiltinPreset, effect.apply, layer.setBlendMode. NO effect.remove, NO delete-ish, NO edit.undo/edit.redo, NO render/export ids (test sweeps the set with /delete|remove|undo|redo|render|export|batch/). Own-property Set membership — "__proto__"/"constructor"/"toString" command ids can never resolve through the prototype chain.
- AUTO_APPLY_MAX_CREDITS = 3, DOCUMENTED CHOICE: ECUX §7.3 says "credit spend above a threshold" without naming a number. 3 credits = comfortably above every 1-4-step low-risk estimate (estimateCredits: 1 step = 1.5, 4 = 3.0 at the low multiplier) and far below render/high-risk spends (renders estimate 12+ in the M5 goldens). Tighten/loosen by business ruling; the constant is the single knob.
- Wiring (routes/chat.ts, minimal additive region inside the plan-event emission seam): the per-user setting is read ONCE per run (awaited before runs.register — the pinned M6-D seam `repos.settings.getUserAutoApply(userId)`), then streamApi.emit wraps every payload through `attachAutoApplyVerdict`: plan payloads get `autoApply: {mode, reason}` stamped, everything else passes through untouched. Defense-in-depth reconstruction: the policy input is rebuilt from the plan event's SERVER fields only (compiledBatch + risk + estimatedCredits — the compiler's own projection); an event missing ANY of the three (M4-shaped, un-compiled, MockRunner) is treated as not-compiled → propose/compile-failed, never fabricating values. The settings read is guarded structurally (`(repos as Repos & {settings?: seam}).settings` + typeof check + try/catch): repo absent in stub boots, or a throwing read (table not applied), degrades to the propose/"setting-unavailable" verdict — never a crash, never auto (pinned on the live wire by nulling the repo on a booted server). Replay (law #4): the stamped verdict is part of the stored log — the same idempotencyKey replays the identical plan event (pinned byte-shape in the test).
- packages/contracts/src/events.ts (additive region only): PLAN_AUTO_APPLY_SCHEMA {mode: AUTO_APPLY_MODE_SCHEMA("auto"|"propose"), reason: string 1..120} + PlanAutoApplyDecision, attached as PLAN_EVENT_SCHEMA.autoApply OPTIONAL — M4 events keep parsing (pinned: a bare M4-shaped plan event parses with autoApply undefined); reason is a stable human-safe policy CODE (the gateway module owns the vocabulary), so contracts stays decoupled from gateway code evolution and clients own their copy per the web boundary law (M5-G's local-duplication precedent). The gateway's AutoApplyDecision is wire-assignable to PlanAutoApplyDecision (compile-time proof in the test). STUDIO_STREAM_EVENT_SCHEMA/api-client/web parse the extension with zero changes (additive optional).
- services/gateway/tests/auto-apply.test.ts (NEW, 40 tests, deterministic, green ×2 consecutive): (A) pure matrix 28 — eligible case; server-values primacy (credits lie 99 → server 2.0 ⇒ auto); each intent edge; all 9 reversible ids auto; boundary 10 steps/3.00 credits; every propose reason individually + first-failing ORDER pins (setting beats risk, risk beats approval, approval beats intent, intent beats command, command beats credits, credits beats steps); command-subset rejections (effect.remove, text.delete, edit.undo/redo, comp.new, layer.newSolid, layer.style.add, prop.setExpression, engine.batch, dunder/prototype ids, mixed-batch poison); credit bounds (3.01/99/NaN/Infinity/negative/non-number hostile compile result); step bounds (11 fails, 0-step malformed fails closed); hostile plan strings ride as data; determinism + no input mutation; copy hygiene (every reason: non-empty, ≤200, no stack/paths/code internals — law #1); constants + the frozen nine ⊆ AGENT_COMMAND_IDS. - D-22 CANDIDATE RULING (recorded per the task; for docs/DECISIONS.md adoption by the orchestrator): "Auto-apply (§7.3, BUILD_PLAN §M5.6) is UI GUIDANCE ONLY in v1. The per-user setting (userSettings.autoApplyEnabled, default false, read server-side per run) flips the plan card's default state to ARMED — mode 'auto' means the card may render an 'Auto-apply enabled' state — but Apply is STILL the user's explicit button press; the client NEVER executes a plan from the plan event alone. Rationale: §M5.6 ships the feature 'off by default' + §9 lists it as the FIRST scope cut, so the trust budget must be earned before any client-side auto-execution; the server verdict (mode/reason) rides the plan event as typed guidance, and any future auto-execution would be a separate, deliberate ruling (and would still route through the same ONE-click-apply pipeline: one atomic engine.batch = one undo step = one version)." Also recorded in the auto-apply.ts module header + the events.ts field doc so the constraint travels with the code.

- EXTENDED the StripeSeam region of services/gateway/src/context.ts — ADDITIVE ONLY (the five existing members keep byte-identical signatures so M6-C's billing.ts stays green). New seam types: StripeSeamResult<T> = {ok:true; data:T} | {ok:false; code:"not_configured"|"not_found"|"invalid_request"|"upstream_error"; reason:string} (reason = fixed human-safe string — no SDK messages, keys, ids, stacks, law #1); SubscriptionSnapshot; TestClockView; StripeSdkSubscriptionLike; StripeSdkLike (structural SDK subset — the REAL SDK satisfies it, proven by createStripeSeam's assignment at typecheck; stripe-node v23 pins API 2026-09-30.endive where current_period_end lives on the subscription ITEM, not the subscription).
- EXACT NEW MEMBER SIGNATURES (M6-C codes against these; full JSDoc in context.ts):
  topUpCheckout(params: {packId: string /* Stripe PRICE id — caller resolves env.stripe.priceIds */; quantity: number; customerId?: string; customerEmail: string; successUrl: string; cancelUrl: string; metadata: Record<string,string>}): Promise<StripeSeamResult<{id: string; url: string}>>   // mode:"payment", payment_intent_data carries metadata
  subscription(customerId: string): Promise<StripeSeamResult<SubscriptionSnapshot>>   // newest sub first; {ok:false, code:"not_found", reason:"no subscription found for customer"} when none
  updateSubscription(customerId: string, params: {planPriceKey: string /* Stripe PRICE id */; cancelAtPeriodEnd?: boolean}): Promise<StripeSeamResult<SubscriptionSnapshot>>   // swaps items[0].price + sets cancel_at_period_end; omitted flag = unchanged; rejected (invalid_request) on canceled subs
  createTestClock(params: {frozenTime: number /* unix seconds */; name?: string; customerId?: string}): Promise<StripeSeamResult<TestClockView>>   // TestClockView = {id, frozenTime, status}
  withTestClock(clockId: string): StripeSeamResult<StripeSeam>   // decorator: NEW customers created through the returned view ride the clock (clocks then propagate to their subscriptions); shares the base seam's seen-set + raw handle; typed not_configured on a live key with NO SDK call
  markEventSeen(id: string): void   // complement of seenEvent: pre-mark without claiming
  SubscriptionSnapshot = {status: "trialing"|"active"|"past_due"|"canceled"|"incomplete"; currentPeriodEnd: number; trialEnd?: number; cancelAtPeriodEnd: boolean; subscriptionId: string; priceId: string|null}   // status mapping: unpaid→past_due (dunning/grace), incomplete_expired/paused/unknown→canceled (unknown never grants)
- REWROTE services/gateway/src/stripe.ts: createStripeSeam(env, options?: {sdk?: StripeSdkLike}) — the SDK is now INJECTABLE (src change I own; tests pass fake.sdk and the production `new Stripe(secretKey)` never runs). Null-when-unconfigured kept (STRIPE_SECRET_KEY unset → null). TYPED ERROR BOUNDARY around EVERY SDK call: extended members → {ok:false, code, reason} classified ONLY by StripeError.type/statusCode (reasons: "stripe <op>: request failed" / "request rejected" / "object not found"); legacy throwing members (checkout/portal/createCustomer — envelope shapes frozen by billing.ts) reject with typed GatewayError SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE 503 "The billing provider is unavailable right now." (raw failure stays server-side in `cause`; never serialized). verifyWebhook now shape-validates the verified payload (toEventLike: id/type/data.object) before the cast. seenEvent/markEventSeen share one in-memory Set per seam instance. Test clocks gated on /^(sk|rk)_test_/.
- NEW services/gateway/tests/helpers/fake-stripe.ts — FakeStripe harness (in-process FakeOpenAIServer-at-the-SDK-seam; deterministic — `now` = fixed epoch 2025-01-01, never Date.now(); zero network). SCRIPTING API: scriptEvent(type, object)→FakeStripeEvent; eventLog (all events in creation order); pendingWebhooks()/nextWebhook()/requeueWebhook(event) (FIFO delivery queue + replay control); signWebhook(event, {timestampSeconds?, secret?})→{payload: Buffer, signature}; signPayload(payload, opts) low-level signer; completeCheckout(sessionId)→events (marks session complete, resolves/creates the customer, creates the trialing subscription for subscription mode — D-4: 14-day trial, 30-day period — emits customer.subscription.created then checkout.session.completed); setPaymentFailing(customerId, bool); advanceTestClock(clockId, frozenTimeSeconds)→events with DETERMINISTIC transitions (trialing→active at trialEnd; renewal +30d per period; failing renewal→past_due; cancel_at_period_end→customer.subscription.deleted at the boundary; past_due dunning = one 30d grace cycle then canceled); queueSdkFailure({type, statusCode?, message?}) — next SDK call throws FakeStripeSdkError; reset(). Webhooks mirror Stripe's `t=<unix>,v1=<hex>` HMAC-SHA256 scheme with the 300s tolerance + multi-v1 list — verified against the production seam's verifyWebhook via fake.sdk. TWO MODES: `fake.sdk` (inject into createStripeSeam — drives the REAL seam) and `fake.seam` (a ready-made full-StripeSeam facade over the same store — M6-C injects it as deps.stripe in route tests; typechecked against the interface).
- NEW services/gateway/tests/stripe-seam.test.ts (29 tests, all <100ms, no DB needed): config (null-when-unconfigured; raw===injected sdk; live-key → typed not_configured test clocks with zero SDK calls asserted on the empty store); signature verification (valid / missing header / tampered payload / wrong secret / 299s-vs-301s tolerance boundary / multi-v1 list / byte-determinism); webhook idempotency (seenEvent claims exactly once — replayed signed bytes verify but dedup; markEventSeen pre-marks without claiming); checkout/topUp/portal/customer happy paths through the REAL seam ← fake sdk (subscription session open with metadata + no sub until completion; topUp quantity-3 pack + customer linkage; portal session; typed-boundary: SDK failure on a legacy member → GatewayError with cause server-side + no SDK text, on an extended member → {ok:false, code:"not_found"} with no leaked ids); subscription lifecycle via test clocks (not_found; D-4 trialing snapshot with CLOCK-anchored timestamps (not wall); trial→active; healthy renewal advances the period; failing renewal→past_due→canceled after the dunning cycle; updateSubscription plan swap + cancelAtPeriodEnd → deleted at the boundary + invalid_request on canceled; not_found on unknown customer); out-of-order delivery (signatures verify independently of order; replayed id dedups) + completeCheckout emission order + requeue/replay; the fake.seam facade pinned (checkout→completeCheckout→subscription trialing; topUp; createTestClock+withTestClock; verifyWebhook+seenEvent/markEventSeen; not_found).
- FINDINGS (report-only, law #12 — not fixed): (1) routes/billing.ts pack flow resolves priceIds[`pack:${packId}`] ?? priceIds[packId] AND reads priceIds[`${packId}:credits`] for the credits metadata — the seam's topUpCheckout takes the PRICE id directly (caller resolves the map once); M6-C should rewire checkout(mode:"payment") → topUpCheckout and keep the credits lookup at the route. (2) The legacy throwing members now reject with 503 SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE on ANY SDK failure (even Stripe 400s) — safe-by-construction; price-id validation stays at the route (billing.ts already does it). (3) seenEvent is in-memory per seam/process — cross-restart/multi-instance webhook idempotency needs M6-D's new webhookEvents repo (migration 0002) as the durable store; the seam interface is left unchanged so routes can layer it. (4) stripe-node v23 (API 2026-09-30.endive): subscription-level current_period_end is GONE — the period lives on subscription items; the real seam + fake both read items.data[0].current_period_end; M6-C must not expect sub.current_period_end. Task ID: M6-I
Agent: general-purpose subagent (M6-I)
Task: BUILD_PLAN §M6 task 3 — the reconciliation job: windowed ledger-vs-usage(-vs-LiteLLM-spend) with zombie holds, report-only default + explicit idempotent true-up, admin route + operator sweep entry point + tests.

Work Log:
- Read the mandatory set in order: AGENTS.md (law), worklog tail (M4-A usage capture/redaction — stream_options.include_usage → committedCredits = ceil(in/1k×rIn + out/1k×rOut) min 1 from MODEL_CATALOG, prompt-logging = chars + sha256 prefix ONLY; M4-K chat-loop harness + ledger-settle notes; M5-K apply-flow (D-20 live-loop pins); M6-A catalog (µc allowances, INT32 finding); M6-D billing repos; M6-E bigint ledger — amount/balance_after are bigint mode:"number", exact ≤2^53), BUILD_PLAN §M6 task 3, PA §8.8, DECISIONS D-12/D-19/D-20, then the surfaces: litellm/runner.ts (usage accumulator + committedCreditsFor), routes/renders.ts + repos/renders.ts, repos/{ledger,runs}.ts, ledger-math.ts (outstandingReserve/planSettlement/deriveIdempotencyKey), env.ts (LITELLM_BASE_URL required / API_KEY / VIRTUAL_KEY optional — NO spend var in the 34-var contract), context.ts (GatewayDeps carries repos, NOT a raw db handle), middleware.ts (idempotency middleware already covers POST /v1/*), routes/credits.ts, docs/deploy/LITELLM.md (§3 master key is ops-only, NEVER app env; §7 spend tables live in litellm-db; NOT deployed on this box) + deploy/litellm/config.yaml (admin spend routes stay enabled, master-key protected), gateway test harness.
- services/gateway/src/reconciliation.ts (NEW, ~1100 lines): reconcileWorkspace(workspaceId, {sinceTs, uptoTs}, deps, options) — pure-ish core over injectable ReconcileSources {ledgerScan (windowed walk), runLedger, runRow, renderScan, balance, appendAdjust}; createRepoReconcileSources(repos) default adapter walks repos.ledger.list/repos.renders.list NEWEST-FIRST with keyset cursors and stops at the first entry older than sinceTs (scanLimit default 5000, truncation flagged in report.scan) — a db-side windowed aggregate is the documented follow-up (M4-K already flagged this for outstandingReserveForWorkspace). Runs discovered from ledger runIds (repos has NO workspace-wide runs listing — listByThread is per-thread only); per-run outstanding via outstandingReserve over the run's full ledger (ledger.list({runId})).
- Report shape (ReconcileReport): workspaceId, window (ISO), mode, ledger {grants, reserves (Σ|reserve|), outstandingReserves (Σ per-run outstanding), commits (Σ|commit|), refunds, adjusts (signed), entryCount}, usage {runs {discovered, settledInWindow, committedCredits}, renders {count, credits}, usageSum}, balance (SUM(amount), D-20), discrepancies[], external {source "none"|"litellm", spendCredits, perModel, unknownModels, error {code, reason}}, action {attempted, applied, replayed, adjustAmount, entryId, balanceAfter, skippedReason}, scan {truncated, scannedEntries}, generatedAt.
- Discrepancy taxonomy (typed union): ledger_usage_mismatch (ledgerCommitSum vs usageSum = runs-table committedCredits of runs with in-window commits + renders-table credits; carries per-run runDrift {runId, ledgerCommits, rowCommitted} for runs whose row ≠ their own ledger commits — the two-sided-drift detail a human reviews before true-upping); zombie_reserve (D-19: outstanding > 0 AND newest reserve older than grace — default 30 min; runStatus incl. "missing" when the run row is gone; NEVER auto-released, even in reconcile mode); external_spend_mismatch (only when a spend source exists; report-only by design — LiteLLM records spend at request time, the ledger at settle time, and M4-A's per-run ceil/min-1 ≠ per-model exact math, so attribution noise is inherent); negative_balance (D-12: must NEVER; critical). balance_chain_broken deliberately NOT implemented — see finding 1.
- True-up (mode:"reconcile", never default): adjustAmount = ledgerCommitSum − usageSum (make the ledger's charged total agree with recorded usage), kind "adjust" via repos.ledger.adjust (NEVER allowNegativeBalance — D-12; a refusing adjust surfaces as action.skippedReason "adjust_refused_by_ledger", balance untouched), metadata {kind: "reconciliation_true_up", note, windowStart/End, ledgerCommitSum, usageSum, delta, counts}; idempotency key = deriveIdempotencyKey("recon", workspaceId, sinceTs.getTime()) — per (workspace, windowStart): same window replays free; a SHIFTED windowStart is a NEW true-up by definition (documented operator-beware: re-true-upping an overlapping window double-adjusts the overlap — review the report-only run first; pinned in tests).
- External seam: SpendSource {kind:"litellm", spendForWindow} NEVER throws (typed SpendFailure {code: unreachable|timeout|unauthorized|upstream_error|bad_response, reason} with fixed human strings); LiteLLMSpendSource GET {baseUrl}/global/spend/models?start_date&end_date, bearer = spend credential, zod-parsed (law #2), 10s AbortSignal.timeout, injectable fetchImpl + logger; per-model credits = spendCreditsForModel (MODEL_CATALOG math, ceil per model aggregate); unknown aliases listed in external.unknownModels (credits 0 — surfaced, never silently billed); redaction = M4-A pattern: key stays in a closure, never in reports/logs; failure logs carry only {code, status?, urlFingerprint: sha256(baseUrl).slice(0,16)}.

- NEW packages/config/src/render-pricing.ts: the single estimate = reserve = commit formula. RENDER_BASE_RATE_UC per OUTPUT second (mp4 100_000 / png-seq 50_000 / prores 400_000 / gif 150_000 / wav 10_000 µc — reserved formats priced for completeness, D-2 gate still refuses them at queue time); RENDER_RESOLUTION_TIERS by LONG EDGE as exact rationals (≤1280 → 1/2, ≤1920 → 1/1, ≤2560 → 2/1, ≤3840 → 4/1, above 3840 = illegal input refused with typed error); RENDER_QUALITY_MULTIPLIERS (draft 3/5, good 1/1, best 7/5); RENDER_MIN_CHARGE_UC 250_000. Documented EXACT order: validate → integer outputMs (millisecond granularity, round-trip epsilon like creditsToMicrocredits) → numerator = base×ms×resNum×qualNum → denominator = 1000×resDen×qualDen → ONE round-half-up division floor((2N+D)/2D) → min-charge clamp. With the v1 constants every µc/ms rate is whole so the single division is always exact — the round-half-up is exactness insurance for future price tables; duration cap 24h keeps every intermediate < 2^53 (exact integers).
- estimateRenderCostUc({ format, outputSeconds, longEdgePx, quality }) → integer µc — THE seam for the gateway (reserve/commit) and the UI (estimate display via microcreditsToCredits). Added estimateRenderCostDetailUc → frozen { totalUc, rawUc, minApplied } AFTER seeing the landed M8-C contracts EstimateResult.minApplied — one shared internal computation (computeRenderCost), so the estimate route can report the clamp WITHOUT re-deriving the formula (the one place drift could creep in). renderOutputSeconds({ durationSeconds, timeFrom?, timeTo? }) = (timeTo ?? duration) − (timeFrom ?? 0) — the pure range→seconds derivation helper for the gateway; full-duration default, typed errors on inverted/empty ranges. Plus RENDER_QUALITY_IDS mirror of the contracts enum, isRenderFormatDeclared, isRenderQualityId; everything deep-frozen; typed VALIDATION_FAILED errors never echo input values.
- plans.ts (additive, no renames): maxConcurrentRenders (trial 1 / starter 1 / pro 3 / studio 8), maxRenderLongEdgePx (1920/1920/3840/3840), renderRetentionDays (3/7/30/90) on PlanDescriptor + catalog rows + Entitlements + entitlementsFor (now a nine-field frozen bundle); renderMinutesRemaining(planId, minutesUsed) helper — clamped-at-zero pure arithmetic, gateway owns enforcement, unknown plans throw the getPlan typed error. renderMinutesPerMonth kept untouched.

- CREATED apps/web/src/components/billing/usage-section.tsx: (1) mounts UsageMeters — creditsUsedMicrocredits = NET commits (gross |commit| − refunds, floored at 0: a refund returns reserved-but-unused credits per §8.8) over the ledger page, vs entitlements.monthlyCreditAllowance; seatsUsed when the page carries a count, else the designed "—"; (2) "Latest renders" per-project Table from renders.list (own first-page fetch, limit 50, race-guarded useUsageRenders hook — the useLedgerPages recipe) grouped by projectId (bounded short-id group headers, the shortRunId precedent), columns Status/Format/Credits/Size, D-11/D-2 label maps mirroring renders-panel (billing-kit self-containment precedent), EmptyState on zero renders, Skeletons on first load, inline error + Try again; (3) commit/refund ledger summary (Committed/Refunded totals) over the SHARED useLedgerPages state the Credit-ledger card already loads — ONE fetch, two surfaces, so the existing ledger pagination tests stay untouched and green.
- HONEST WINDOW (manifest: "within the current period WHEN billing.state carries period bounds — otherwise label the window honestly 'Latest ledger entries'"): verified billing.state carries currentPeriodEnd ONLY (routes/billing.ts:359-395, contracts billing.ts:142-158 — NO period start), so the wire does NOT carry period bounds and the "this cycle" arm cannot fire. All windows are therefore labeled "Latest renders" / "Latest ledger entries" (+ a "N newest ledger rows" count line); nothing claims a billing cycle. The meter input is likewise the latest-window net commits — real ledger numbers, honestly labeled, never an invented cycle bound.

UsageMeters renders a storage row (and a render-minutes row) by design and W2-08 requires mounting it, so those rows are fed the component's DESIGNED unknown (NaN → "— of 200 GB", its hostile-input arm, usage-meters.test.tsx:167-190) — never a fabricated 0. The gap is stated in UI copy (usage-meter-gap-note: "Storage and render-minute usage aren't metered yet — plan limits are enforced per upload and per render"). Gateway follow-up owed by its owner: a used-bytes aggregate read before any real storage meter.

- scripts/dev-sqlite.sh: seed() now resolves pnpm via `command -v pnpm || corepack pnpm` (plain pnpm is missing on this box); stale "seed not sqlite-aware yet" caveats rewritten (seed is sqlite-aware and green since W3-A; a failure is now a bug, still non-fatal).
