- Files: packages/db/src/{db,tables,sqlite-bootstrap}.ts, packages/db/src/repos/* (all 17 repos + shared.ts), packages/db/tests/sqlite/dev-server.test.ts (NEW), scripts/dev-sqlite.sh. Nothing outside packages/db/** + scripts/dev-sqlite.sh touched (gateway reached only through its public @studio/db seam).
- Key decisions: (1) tablesFor in EVERY repo rather than an sqlite now() function shim — the shim would mix ISO-string and integer-ms timestamp storage and corrupt ordering/comparisons; (2) execute() alias on the sqlite handle keeps the signature-preserving seam honest without editing gateway-owned code; (3) sqlite unique violations map onto the same typed CONFLICT path as 23505; (4) dev sqlite databases stay throwaway (bootstrap twin DDL only; pg migrations never run against the twin).
- Test counts: @studio/db 140 passed / 71 skipped (PG-gated integration) / 0 failed; sqlite dev-server suite 7/7 green.
- Gateway smoke verdict: GREEN — 200 + db:true on file:.local/studio-dev.db with MOCK_MODEL=true.
- Blockers: none. Notes for owners: (a) gateway close() calls db.$client.end() — on sqlite that throws inside its own try/catch (harmless, but a dialect-aware close via @studio/db would be cleaner — gateway owner's call); (b) packages/db has no README.md though sqlite-bootstrap.ts references one (pre-existing doc drift); (c) drizzle/meta/*.json format + noConsole warnings pre-exist at HEAD.

Task ID: W5-01
Agent: W5-01 (retry — prior attempt ran out of turns)

Task: Boot the REAL stack (gateway :8787 + web :3000) on the SQLite dev DB and prove the core flows; fix what is broken; write the reproducible recipe.

Work Log:
- Read env law: packages/config/src/env.ts is the single source of truth — required vars are APP_URL, APP_ENV, DATABASE_URL, ASSET_MAX_BYTES, PROJECT_MAX_BYTES, STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET, STRIPE_PRICE_IDS, AUTH_SECRET (≥32 chars — NOT BETTER_AUTH_SECRET), AUTH_GOOGLE_{ID,SECRET}, AUTH_GITHUB_{ID,SECRET}, LITELLM_BASE_URL, LITELLM_API_KEY, CHAT_MAX_STEPS. MOCK_MODEL=true is legal in local (rejected only in production); SQLite DATABASE_URL is local-only. Gateway does NOT auto-load .env — vars must be in the environment.
- Found a stale pre-booted stack from the prior attempt holding :8787 (its env was wrong → sign-in with Origin header 403 INVALID_ORIGIN). Killed it; with the canonical env the same request is 200 — not a code bug, a stale-process bug.
- Boot bug found + fixed: exporting PORT=8787 for the gateway also reaches `next dev`, which honors PORT and steals 8787 (web logs "Local: http://localhost:8787", gateway dies EADDRINUSE). Fix: scope PORT to the gateway launch only (`nohup env PORT=8787 …`) in scripts/dev-stack.sh; documented the trap in a comment.
- Verified (curl, real stack): GET /v1/health → {"status":"ok","db":true}; POST /v1/auth/sign-in/email via the web /v1 proxy with Origin header → 200 + set-cookie (better-auth.session_token); GET /v1/me with cookie → real user JSON (Demo User, org_demo owner membership, trial plan, 1000 credits) — direct :8787 and via :3000/v1 proxy both work; GET /v1/projects (x-workspace-id: org_demo) → seeded Demo Project row. Public pages /, /sign-in, /pricing → 200. Authed pages /w, /w/projects, /w/billing, /w/settings/members → 200 with real markup + testids (app-shell, account-menu, dashboard-greeting, projects-card-prj_demo, billing-credit-balance, capability-*). Unauth /w → 307 to /sign-in?next=%2Fw.
- Note for future agents: an early AUTH_REQUIRED on /v1/me was a curl cookie-jar domain artifact (cookie set by localhost:3000 not sent to 127.0.0.1:8787) — always match hosts when testing cookies.
- New scripts/dev-stack.sh (33 lines, start|stop; logs .local/{gateway,web}-dev.log) + cross-link added to scripts/dev-sqlite.sh header. Gates: bash -n both scripts; biome has no .sh surface (no-op); no TS files touched → no typecheck/vitest delta. All servers killed; ports 3000/8787 verified free.

Env recipe (exact, APP_ENV=local):
  APP_URL=http://localhost:3000 APP_ENV=local MOCK_MODEL=true
  DATABASE_URL=file:/home/z/my-project/.local/studio-dev.db
  AUTH_SECRET=dev-local-secret-0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef (any ≥32 chars)
  AUTH_GOOGLE_ID=replace-me AUTH_GOOGLE_SECRET=replace-me AUTH_GITHUB_ID=replace-me AUTH_GITHUB_SECRET=replace-me
  STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET=whsec_replace_me STRIPE_PRICE_IDS='{"starter":"price_starter","pro":"price_pro","studio":"price_studio"}'
  LITELLM_BASE_URL=http://localhost:4000 LITELLM_API_KEY=sk-litellm-replace-me
  ASSET_MAX_BYTES=524288000 PROJECT_MAX_BYTES=262144000 CHAT_MAX_STEPS=50
  (web) NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL=http://localhost:3000 GATEWAY_INTERNAL_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8787
  Boot: `bash scripts/dev-stack.sh start`; stop: `bash scripts/dev-stack.sh stop`.

Stage Summary: W5-01 complete — the real stack boots on the SQLite dev DB and every core flow (health, email sign-in via the /v1 proxy, session'd /v1/me, workspace-scoped projects API, all four authed /w pages, auth redirect gate) is verified green. One real bug fixed (PORT collision: next dev stealing 8787 when PORT is exported); recipe committed as scripts/dev-stack.sh. Remaining broken: nothing found in the verified surface; untested here (out of scope, needs a running LiteLLM): real model chat runs — MOCK_MODEL=true covers the offline path only.

- Teardown: SIGTERM → graceful "gateway_shutdown" log, port :8790 verified free, no leftover processes; removed .local/tmp-gateway.db{,-wal,-shm} and the cookie jar (session-token hygiene). Left pre-existing :8787/:3000 processes and tmp-w1a1.db (another agent's) untouched.

Stage Summary:
- Boot status: HEALTHY — gateway boots on SQLite (W1-02 dual-dialect seam works through createDb → better-sqlite3 twin + bootstrapSqlite DDL at open); zero gateway code changes required.

- No blockers in packages/db — seed + sqlite seam worked as designed. Two operational caveats for future agents: (1) :8787 is occupied by a stale gateway from a prior session — either reuse it or boot with PORT=<alt>; (2) run boot+probe+kill inside ONE Bash invocation or the sandbox reaps the server.

## v1 loop readiness (§1, step by step)

| Loop step | Verdict | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| landing | **WORKS** | (marketing)/page.tsx 11 sections + real captures (demo-loop.webm 8 s 1280×720 from live editor, editor-frame.png 1600×900) |
| sign up | **WORKS** | (auth)/sign-up + sign-up-form; routes to /onboarding; email verification honestly disabled (no mail transport) |
| create workspace | **WORKS** | onboarding-flow 3 steps (W3-E); workspace create + slug 409 arm; membership gate self-heals |
| start trial (card) | **PARTIAL** | POST /v1/billing/trial + trial-step UI + card-required + abuse gates all real; proven against FakeStripe in 9 billing-e2e journeys; NOT exercised against real test-mode Stripe (no keys on box); card-fingerprint provisioning seam is FakeStripe-modeled |
| dashboard | **WORKS** | (app)/w/(shell)/page.tsx + dashboard-view (W2-03 landed; stub replaced); shell rail/top-bar/switcher/⌘K palette/offline/toasts |
| create/open project | **WORKS** | new-project-dialog + import-project-dialog (sha256+upload+409 arms); projects list; editor RSC page resolves by slug, cross-tenant 404 |
| editor loads (unmodified EffectCraft at /editor/, iframe + window.effectcraft) | **WORKS** | 76.2 MB wasm dist served same-origin; EditorHost iframe ?empty; bridge attach + readiness handshake; REAL browser boot PROVEN on this box (W5-02 captures were recorded from the live editor); p75 timings unmeasured |
| upload/import assets | **WORKS** | assets routes (two-phase presign, MIME sniff, SHA-256 dedupe, quotas W1-c) + hub assets surface + load.ts registers by virtual path |
| chat (plan/preview/apply/undo, engine.batch atomic, one undo step) | **WORKS** (model env tail) | Full M4+M5 path real: SSE + client tools + server plan compiler + plan card + ONE atomic engine.batch + ONE version + µc commit + undo/reapply-gated; MOCK_MODEL=true in dev; real-model path proven via fake-OpenAI E2E (chat-loop/apply-flow); deployed LiteLLM needed for live first-token |
| version saved | **WORKS** | autosave controller (30 s dirty ticks, Cmd/Ctrl+S, before-close, agent-apply) + immutable versions + 409 conflict recovery |
| close/reopen intact | **WORKS** (engine-level) | load.ts restores active comp/playhead/selection; proven in unit/integration tests; browser reload E2E is CI-owed (no dedicated M3 spec) |
| render/export | **PARTIAL — BROKEN ON THIS BOX** | API+worker+UI all real and journey-proven (render-e2e 9 tests, golden 1080p 10 s in 59 s) BUT effectcraft-cli binary is absent on the current box (sandbox reset removed it; no cargo to rebuild) → live renders fail honestly RENDER_CLI_MISSING; 16 CLI-gated tests skip |
| download | **WORKS** | signed resultUrl only while completed+objectKey; real MP4 bytes proven in render-e2e (ftyp box checks); ZIP for png-seq |
| billing ledger shows credits used | **WORKS** | µc-unified ledger (bigint, migration 0003) + /w/billing ledger table with cursor pages + usage section (renders table, net-commit meters) + reconciliation job |

**Bottom line:** the v1 loop is ~90% real and locally bootable (dev-stack.sh on SQLite; gateway+web+editor verified). The two environmental breaks are (a) no real Stripe keys → trial-card step dev-degrades to typed 400, and (b) no effectcraft-cli binary → render step honestly fails. The two systemic debts are (a) NO CI — zero of the 6 Playwright specs have ever run in CI, and (b) perf budgets almost entirely unmeasured. M10 is the open milestone.

## TOP-20 build list for Waves 2–5 (critical-path ordered)

1. **Rebuild effectcraft-cli** (install Rust toolchain → `cargo build --release -p effectcraft-cli` in editor/, chunked foreground calls per box law) — un-blocks the live render step of the v1 loop + un-skips 16 CLI-gated worker/gateway tests. (W1-09's owed task; highest single impact.)
2. **Author `.github/workflows/ci.yml`** — web (typecheck/lint/test/build), gateway+db (typecheck/tests with Postgres service), worker (units + CLI-gated), editor (cargo test/clippy + wasm build at pin), licence check (scripts/check-licenses.mjs), Playwright job using apps/web/e2e/README.md boot recipe. Every "CI-owed" proof hangs on this.
3. **Run the full Playwright suite once in CI/staging** — chat.spec, m5-agent-edits (after #4), m7-collab, m8-renders, m9-visual, m9-axe — then fix first-run reds (320 px h-scroll risk, axe findings, baseline diffs).
4. **Flip m5-agent-edits.spec.ts test.fixme → test** (~line 435; the K-1 applyPorts host wiring landed at M5-M7 closeout, so the journey is now unblocked) + **write the dedicated M3 editor spec** (open→edit→save→reload→verify comp tree) — closes the M3.8/M5.8 E2E holes.
5. **Performance measurement wave** on a reference-class machine: editor-ready p75 warm/cold, chat first-token, apply p75, preview-frame 720p, API read/write p95 — record each in docs/performance.md; fix or waive per §6.
6. **Deploy LiteLLM** (docker compose or venv per docs/deploy/LITELLM.md) with real keys → MOCK_MODEL=false live verification + first-token measurement + virtual-key minting drill.
7. **Real Stripe test-mode wiring drill**: keys + price ids, card-fingerprint provisioning seam (AUD-3 #6 — the one never-landed fix), test-clock trial→renewal→past-due→cancel on staging; resolve BUSINESS-PENDING pricing numbers.
8. **Load tests** per §7.1: 50 concurrent editor sessions, 10 concurrent renders, 100 chat runs/hour (k6/autocannon); record numbers + waivers.
9. **Security review execution**: run docs/security/checklist.md item-by-item + ZAP passive scan + close findings; sign off threat-model.md; verify §7.3 list (cross-tenant, IDOR, signed URLs, SSRF, polyglots, injection, webhook replay, render escape, rate limits).
10. **Observability stand-up** per ADR-0005/M10.5: metrics + dashboards + alerts (5xx, queue depth, render failures, credit mismatch, wasm load errors); trace sampling; wire the reconciliation sweep to an alert.
11. **Backup + restore drill** on a production-shaped environment (RB-06) and record it in docs/runbooks/drill-log.md; verify render-output retention sweeps under real load.
12. **Editor pin rollback drill** (§8.4): previous wasm artifact + previous app release, open a project saved by the new pin first; document the tested path.
13. **evals/prompts corpus**: expand 12 golden fixtures to ≥30 prompts across the 10 §7.2 categories + a scoring harness (plan validity / apply success / visual sanity / undo / credits / latency; targets ≥90%/≥95%).
14. **Email transport seam** (SMTP/Resend) → enable email verification + invite emails (verify-email page is an honest degrade today).
15. **Storage usage read** (used-bytes aggregate gateway route) + real storage meter on the billing page (currently entitlement-only).
16. **Share-page frame stepping** (M7 v1 gap) — either a per-frame render path for anonymous users or an explicit documented waiver in M7.md.
17. **Docs debt**: write docs/phases/M1.md + M2.md evidence files; retire/replace stale pre-pivot docs/phases/{phase-exit-audit.md, acceptance-matrix.md}; sweep BUILD_PLAN status line + README for the post-M9 truth.
18. **Scale-out seams**: SSE replay/RunRegistry on Valkey (multi-instance posture) or a documented single-instance waiver for beta; same for the in-memory rate limiter.
19. **Wasm dist re-tune** on a ≥16 GB box (codegen-units=1 + wasm-opt -O2) toward ≈58 MB/10–12 MB brotli (performance.md waiver), then re-run the editor-unchanged baselines.
20. **Private-beta prep**: final pricing + legal pass, status-page ops wiring, 10–20 design-partner onboarding + instrumentation, P0/P1 triage board, crash-free/apply-success/refund metrics gates (§M10.7).

Critical-path lens (M0→M1→M2→M3→M4→M5→M10): items 1–4 are the proof debt on the already-built critical path; 5–12 are M10 hardening (the gate to private beta); 13–20 are corpus/compliance/polish. Nothing on the critical path is structurally missing — M10 execution is the remaining milestone.

Agent: general-purpose (W3-C10)
Task: Build the §7.2 evals corpus — evals/prompts/ with 40 golden agent prompts + a vitest-runnable scoring harness (schema-integrity suite green on-box, live-model runs skip-guarded behind EVALS_LIVE=1).

Work Log:
- Recon (no edits): docs/BUILD_PLAN.md §7.2 (10 prompt categories + 6 scoring dims: plan validity, apply success, visual sanity, undo success, credits accuracy, latency; targets ≥90% valid plans, ≥95% apply on valid) and §7.1 Evals row; services/gateway/src/litellm/prompt.ts (SYSTEM_PROMPT shape, §5.4 primer, planInstruction §8.6 EditPlan grammar); services/gateway/src/plan-compiler.ts header (tool→command translation table, issue codes incl. too_many_commands/empty_step, D-1 caps: plan ≤20 steps, batch ≤100 commands); packages/contracts/src/command-registry.ts (37 curated engine commands — no camera/arrange/delete agent surface); services/gateway/tests/golden-plans.ts + golden-prompts.test.ts (12 fixtures: prompt + userContext + scriptedModelPlan + expected compiled commands/risk/credits — learned the M5-S/G-1..G-8 flip history and the server-recomputed risk/credits semantics); packages/contracts edit-plan.ts (PLAN_INTENT_SCHEMA ×8, PLAN_RISK_SCHEMA ×3, estimateCredits = (1 + steps×0.5) × multiplier, reserveFor = est×1.2 min 1) and tools.ts/write-tools.ts (TOOL_IDS 5 read + 8 write, wire form studio.*).
- Created evals/prompts/manifest.json — ONE manifest, 40 prompts (§7.2 floor is 30), schema: id (ev-NNN-kebab), category (16), prompt text, expectedBehavior {type: plan|clarify|confirm|explain|refuse|reject|contain, intent?, description, expectedTools?, forbiddenTools?, mayUseTools?}, risk (post-recompute tier; high ⇒ confirmation), requiresConfirmation, fixtures {project, activeComp, layers[{name,type,detail?}], selection, playheadSeconds, creditBalance}. Counts by category: create-title-comp 3, animate-transform 4, effect-add-tune 3, brand-colors 3, reorganize-rename 2, lower-third 3, camera-3d 2, ambiguous-clarify 3, destructive-confirm 3, impossible-explain 3 (all 10 §7.2 categories) + extras multi-layer-edit 2, keyframe-precision 2, undo-after-apply 2, credits-refusal 2, oversized-plan 1, injection-defense 2. By golden type: 24 plan / 5 explain / 3 clarify / 3 confirm / 2 refuse / 1 reject / 2 contain; risk 28 low / 9 medium / 3 high. Semantics follow the real pipeline: renames ride set_properties path "name"→layer.rename (G-7/D-21), z-order/camera/delete have NO write tool (explain, premise-guarded), undo_run takes no args (law #6), save_version is annotation-only (G-5), apply_preset floors risk to medium, keyframe prompts demand seconds-not-timecode (§5.4), refusal premises pinned to the real credit math (1-step low plan = 1.5 est / 1.8 reserve vs balances 0 and 1).
- Created evals/harness/: types.ts (PromptCategory taxonomy + REQUIRED_CATEGORIES, ExpectedBehavior, PromptFixtures, GoldenPrompt — dependency-free), manifest.ts (loader + validateManifest() integrity checker resolving tool ids/intents/risks against the REAL contracts via read-only relative imports ../../packages/contracts/src/* — drift-checked, no frozen copy; premiseFacts() guards no-camera/no-arrange/no-delete-tool + minPlanCredits/minPlanReserve; unfundablePrompts()), score.ts (six §7.2 dimension stubs — scoreValidity/Apply/Visual/Undo/Credits/Latency, pure functions over an ObservedRun transcript; aggregate() with VALIDITY_TARGET 0.9 / APPLY_TARGET 0.95 and apply-denominator = valid plans only; §6 budgets first-token 1500ms / plan-validation 100ms; runLiveSuite() skip-guarded behind EVALS_LIVE=1 with the requires-LiteLLM contract + typed not-implemented transport; assertCorpusIntegrity() fail-closed helper), manifest.test.ts (the hermetic suite).
- Created evals/README.md (how to run, the 40-prompt coverage table, the 6-dim rubric with targets, current state: harness skeleton green / live-model runs owed to deployed LiteLLM with chat-loop.test.ts named as the transport template; how to add a prompt).

- allowedDevOrigins verification (prescribed command): `PORT=3100 next dev` + curl → full SSR HTML served at http://127.0.0.1:3100/, zero cross-site/blocked/allowedDevOrigins warnings in the dev log, server stopped cleanly. Deeper proof: playwright probes — DIRECT http://127.0.0.1:3000 now HYDRATES (2 __react* keys on document, eye-toggle canary flips, "[HMR] connected") — the loopback-IP origin that Next 16 blocks without the allowlist.
- EDGE FOLLOW-UP (root-caused, fix OUTSIDE my ownership — playwright.config.ts is not mine): the m9-visual billing sweep through the config's own edge (127.0.0.1:3100) still fails at the sign-in hydration canary. Proven NOT a config/app issue: through the edge all 66 resources are 200 with byte-identical bodies (HTML diffs only in the per-request `_R_` nonce; a fetched chunk cmps identical), zero console/page errors, zero pending/failed requests — yet zero __react* keys. Root cause: EDGE_PROXY_JS (the inline node:http proxy) has NO `upgrade` handler, so the Turbopack HMR websocket dies at the edge and Next 16.4 dev never hydrates. Proven by experiment: same proxy + a raw TCP `upgrade` tunnel to :3000 → reactKeys=2, "[HMR] connected", eye-toggle works. EXACT FIX for the e2e owner: add to EDGE_PROXY_JS's server `server.on("upgrade", (req, socket, head) => { const up = net.connect(WEB_PORT, "127.0.0.1", () => { up.write([`${req.method} ${req.url} HTTP/1.1`, ...Object.entries(req.headers).map(([k,v]) => `${k}: ${v}`), "", ""].join("\r\n")); if (head?.length) up.write(head); up.pipe(socket); socket.pipe(up); }); up.on("error", () => socket.destroy()); socket.on("error", () => up.destroy()); });` (requires `require("net")`). Both fixes are jointly required: allowedDevOrigins (landed) unblocks /_next at loopback-IP origins; the WS tunnel unblocks the HMR socket through the edge.
- Gates: `next typegen` ✓; `tsc --noEmit` ✓ clean; web vitest 123 files / 1532 tests ALL GREEN (was 1531 + my 1 new test) including the reworked chat-panel-slot + billing page tests; biome clean on every file I touched. Work Log:
- Continuation inventory: HEAD had moved past 250a94b — 3a766a3 (UUID-named devDep/commit: wasm re-tune artifacts 76.2MB→49,353,339 + e2e probe scripts + demo fixture) and 9f3dcc2 (wave-1 6-agent completion wave) already on origin/main; working tree carried the W2-C/W2-E docs flips + W2-D-flagged boot-and-measure.sh hardening + W2-B's m7-collab.spec.ts first-live rework — W2-B never appended its worklog entry (identified by diff forensics: the spec's own comments name W2-B and the 08:44 UTC first-live run). boot-and-measure.sh reviewed as orchestrator (corepack→plain pnpm PATH law + watch-free tsx boot, matching W1-E's e2e-run.sh hardening) → accepted into the release set.
- THE FIX (real app-side bug from W2-B's first live run, documented "kept RED as evidence"): the server-side comments `resolved` filter was row-level, splitting threads on fresh fetches — a resolved root returned without its replies under ?resolved=true (the RED reload leg) and unresolved replies orphaned under ?resolved=false. Root-gating evidence: comments-panel.tsx renders the Resolve control only on roots (isRoot), so the thread's resolved state IS its root's. Fix in packages/db/src/repos/comments.ts: threadRootResolvedAt CASE walking to the root (parent → grandparent, bounded by MAX_COMMENT_DEPTH=3), applied to BOTH listThreaded and list.
- Implementation history (honest record): v1 used drizzle aliasedTable + two leftJoins — runtime verified correct (TableAliasProxyHandler re-proxies columns → SQL renders "cmt_parent"."col") but the join builder generics collapse on the dual-dialect table union (tablesFor returns pg OR sqlite), typing rows `never` (TS2339 ×2); v2 cast the proxies (tables.ts `as unknown as` precedent) — still `never` (the collapse is the leftJoin itself on the union db handle); v3 (landed) dropped the joins entirely — the root walk lives in the WHERE clause as correlated subqueries over aliased inner copies of the SAME table, safe per the tables.ts seam contract (identical table AND column names across dialects). One slip caught by the gates: v1's import cleanup removed `isNotNull`, still used by unresolve() — esbuild-vitest does not typecheck, so it surfaced as a 500 in the reopen test, NOT at compile time; fixed (import restored) and lesson recorded.
- Regression pins: services/gateway/tests/comments.test.ts +3 (resolved thread returns whole under both filter states; open thread whole under Open; depth-3 two-hop walk) — 28/28 green on sqlite; packages/db/tests/integration/repos.test.ts +1 (repo-level, listThreaded + list, PG-gated); services/gateway/tests/collab-e2e.test.ts journey-3 D-15 filter test RE-PINNED to thread-aware expectations with a dated note (its old assertions pinned the bug itself: length 1 = root-only, and "replies stay open threads of their own" = the orphan mirror gap).
- E2E proof on the committed 49.4MB dist (scripts/e2e-run.sh, full stack): m7-collab reload-persistence leg — run 1 FAILED at the FINAL assertion only: strict-mode violation, thread-wide anchor-chip locator resolved to 2 elements (root + reply, each with a comp chip) — the DOM proving the thread is whole; locator scoped to the root row with a dated note; run 2 PASSED (29.9s). Every DATA assertion in the leg is unchanged.
- Gate ledger (final, on the release tree): biome check . 707 files clean + web lint 308 clean | turbo typecheck --force 10/10 (next typegen first) | db vitest sqlite 140 passed/72 skipped | gateway vitest sqlite 810 passed/9 skipped (baseline 807+9 + 3 new; one fresh scratch DB per run per the W2-D convention) | web vitest 1533/1533 | m7 e2e reload leg 1 passed. Postgres not startable on this box (no pg binaries/service found; port 5432 closed) — the PG-gated integration test remains CI/staging-owned.
